- **AVQ-006-001** Do planted feet remain fixed against the ground during a hold? **Failure hypothesis:** Root movement causes visible foot sliding. **Check or fix:** Track sole contact points across the hold. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-002** Does the hand meet the prop before the prop moves? **Failure hypothesis:** The object responds before physical contact. **Check or fix:** Inspect approach and first-motion frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-003** Does the grip maintain a consistent attachment point? **Failure hypothesis:** Held prop drifts through the palm. **Check or fix:** Track local hand-prop offsets through motion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-004** Is the prop released before its independent trajectory begins? **Failure hypothesis:** Constraint persists into the throw. **Check or fix:** Inspect release frame and neighboring transforms. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-005** Does a receiving hand close around the arriving object? **Failure hypothesis:** Handoff looks like a teleport between owners. **Check or fix:** Animate contact, grip and release in order. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-006** Are fingertips penetrating rigid surfaces visibly? **Failure hypothesis:** Contact solver places anatomy inside the prop. **Check or fix:** Inspect close contact frames and adjust offsets. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-007** Does a carried object rotate plausibly with the wrist? **Failure hypothesis:** Prop orientation is detached from the grip. **Check or fix:** Compare wrist and object rotations across the carry. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-008** Is the object's center of mass supported by the grasp? **Failure hypothesis:** Heavy prop hangs from an implausible pinch. **Check or fix:** Change grip, support or stylized weight cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-009** Does the character react to the load being picked up? **Failure hypothesis:** Object weight has no effect on posture. **Check or fix:** Add anticipatory brace and weight transfer. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-010** Are both feet actually supported on a narrow platform? **Failure hypothesis:** Pose places a sole beyond the supporting edge. **Check or fix:** Check contact regions from the final camera and geometry. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-011** Does a prop preserve its dimensions while changing owners? **Failure hypothesis:** Scaling hides an awkward handoff. **Check or fix:** Compare object bounds before and after transfer. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-012** Is a resting object tangent to rather than floating above its surface? **Failure hypothesis:** Grounding gap makes the prop hover. **Check or fix:** Inspect contact shadow and geometric gap. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-013** Does a rolling object rotate at a plausible travel rate? **Failure hypothesis:** Translation and rotation contradict contact. **Check or fix:** Compare circumference travel with angular rotation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-014** Do dragged objects leave their contact point on the surface? **Failure hypothesis:** Drag path passes through or above the floor. **Check or fix:** Project the path onto the intended support plane. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-015** Does a hinged prop rotate around its visible hinge? **Failure hypothesis:** Door or sign pivots around an arbitrary center. **Check or fix:** Mark hinge position and inspect rotation arcs. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-016** Does a mouth-held object align with the actual mouth? **Failure hypothesis:** Prop floats beside the face. **Check or fix:** Track mouth anchor and occlusion ordering. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-017** Are duplicated props tracked as distinct persistent objects? **Failure hypothesis:** A single item becomes several during crowd action. **Check or fix:** Audit ownership and object-ID transitions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-018** Does the original important prop remain identifiable among copies? **Failure hypothesis:** Payoff loses the object it was built around. **Check or fix:** Preserve a distinctive feature and ownership trace. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-019** Is a pocketed object visibly stowed rather than simply removed? **Failure hypothesis:** Disappearance breaks continuity. **Check or fix:** Stage the insertion or show an unambiguous occlusion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-020** Does a prop's shadow move with its actual position? **Failure hypothesis:** Shadow remains behind after pickup. **Check or fix:** Bind shadow behavior to the same object state. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-021** Are handles and grip surfaces facing the usable direction? **Failure hypothesis:** Characters hold inaccessible parts of tools. **Check or fix:** Check affordances against the intended use. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-022** Does a flexible prop bend where force is applied? **Failure hypothesis:** Deformation originates away from the contact. **Check or fix:** Align bend region with grip and resistance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-023** Are surface contacts maintained during camera movement? **Failure hypothesis:** Screen-space fixes fail when the view changes. **Check or fix:** Recheck contacts from all used cameras. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-024** Does a stack of props have believable support relationships? **Failure hypothesis:** Upper items balance on empty space. **Check or fix:** Audit support polygons and overlap. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-025** Does collision response occur at the visible impact moment? **Failure hypothesis:** Reaction begins before or after contact. **Check or fix:** Compare impact frame to motion and sound onset. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-026** Can viewers see that the character still owns the key item? **Failure hypothesis:** Important possession is hidden for too long. **Check or fix:** Show a clear possession cue before the payoff. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-027** Does the prop intersect costume or anatomy during turns? **Failure hypothesis:** Attachment works only in the front view. **Check or fix:** Inspect full turn silhouettes and internal overlaps. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-028** Are props placed consistently relative to persistent landmarks? **Failure hypothesis:** Set dressing drifts between shots. **Check or fix:** Compare landmark-relative coordinates and encoded frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-029** Does the pickup path avoid passing through another object? **Failure hypothesis:** Hand or prop clips through nearby scenery. **Check or fix:** Check the swept motion envelope. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-030** Are contact shadows appropriate after an object becomes airborne? **Failure hypothesis:** Grounded shadow remains glued beneath flight. **Check or fix:** Adjust shadow offset and softness with height. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-031** Does a tool's output begin at its visible working end? **Failure hypothesis:** Effect originates from an unrelated point. **Check or fix:** Anchor output to the active tip or nozzle. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-006-032** Would a close contact insert clarify the action better than more labels? **Failure hypothesis:** Tiny interaction carries too much narrative burden. **Check or fix:** Test a brief readable contact shot. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D007 silhouette and anatomy

- **AVQ-007-001** Can the key pose be recognized as a filled black silhouette? **Failure hypothesis:** Overlapping limbs conceal the action. **Check or fix:** Review silhouette-only key poses. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-002** Are left and right limbs distinguishable during crossings? **Failure hypothesis:** Limbs swap identity visually. **Check or fix:** Use depth, spacing or differentiated contour. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-003** Does the joint bend in the intended anatomical direction? **Failure hypothesis:** Rig orientation produces inverted elbows or knees. **Check or fix:** Inspect bend extrema in the final view. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-004** Does the head remain attached during squash or rotation? **Failure hypothesis:** Deformation opens an unintended neck gap. **Check or fix:** Check head-neck overlap through extreme poses. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-005** Are limb lengths consistent unless deliberate deformation is clear? **Failure hypothesis:** Uncontrolled stretching changes body proportions. **Check or fix:** Compare segment lengths across key poses. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-006** Do feet have enough shape to communicate their orientation? **Failure hypothesis:** Featureless feet make planted direction ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Add a readable toe or sole cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-007** Are hands readable at the delivered size? **Failure hypothesis:** Finger detail collapses into noise. **Check or fix:** Simplify hand shapes and enlarge important gestures. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-008** Does a character's tail emerge from a coherent attachment point? **Failure hypothesis:** Tail floats or changes anatomical origin. **Check or fix:** Track tail root through turns. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-009** Are ears visibly attached throughout expressive motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Ear pivots detach from the skull. **Check or fix:** Inspect bases at widest rotations. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-010** Does the mouth remain on the face during perspective changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Facial features slide independently of head volume. **Check or fix:** Use consistent face-space projection. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-011** Are eyes aligned to the same implied head orientation? **Failure hypothesis:** Eye placement contradicts skull rotation. **Check or fix:** Compare eye line and face contour. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-012** Does the silhouette distinguish the character from nearby props? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar shapes obscure identity. **Check or fix:** Change contour rhythm or local spacing. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-013** Are thin limbs lost after encoding? **Failure hypothesis:** Line width falls below stable display resolution. **Check or fix:** Inspect encoded limbs at intended screen size. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-014** Does a bent torso retain believable structural continuity? **Failure hypothesis:** Body folds at an arbitrary point. **Check or fix:** Place curvature around the intended spine or hinge. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-015** Are contact poses clear without internal line detail? **Failure hypothesis:** Interior strokes rescue an unreadable outline. **Check or fix:** Open the silhouette around the contact. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-016** Does the character preserve recognizable proportions in perspective? **Failure hypothesis:** Foreshortening is applied inconsistently. **Check or fix:** Compare near and far body segments coherently. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-017** Do shoulder attachments permit the demonstrated arm range? **Failure hypothesis:** Arms rotate through the torso. **Check or fix:** Review shoulder clearance during reach arcs. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-018** Are knees visually separated from the ground during crouches? **Failure hypothesis:** Bent legs merge into an indistinct base. **Check or fix:** Adjust pose spacing and camera elevation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-019** Does clothing preserve rather than obscure essential joint action? **Failure hypothesis:** Costume hides the mechanism of the gesture. **Check or fix:** Simplify folds around active joints. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-020** Are extra apparent limbs created by motion trails? **Failure hypothesis:** Effects make one actor look anatomically duplicated. **Check or fix:** Reduce trail opacity or separate trail styling. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-021** Does a side-view animal show a coherent leg pairing? **Failure hypothesis:** Gait reads as an impossible limb count. **Check or fix:** Track near and far leg silhouettes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-022** Are facial proportions stable across expressions? **Failure hypothesis:** Expression deformation accidentally changes identity. **Check or fix:** Compare neutral and extreme landmark relationships. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-023** Does the pose communicate balance from its silhouette alone? **Failure hypothesis:** Center of mass lies outside support without explanation. **Check or fix:** Align body mass with the support base. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-024** Are extreme poses intentional rather than single-frame rig failures? **Failure hypothesis:** Transient deformation looks broken. **Check or fix:** Review frames around acceleration peaks. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-025** Does the character retain volume during squash and stretch? **Failure hypothesis:** Deformation arbitrarily gains or loses mass. **Check or fix:** Compare approximate body area or volume. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-026** Are line intersections creating unintended facial expressions? **Failure hypothesis:** Contours form accidental brows or mouths. **Check or fix:** Inspect small facial regions at delivery size. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-027** Does an occluded limb reappear on the correct side? **Failure hypothesis:** Depth changes imply anatomical swapping. **Check or fix:** Track limb identity through overlap intervals. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-028** Are articulated cutout seams hidden in normal poses? **Failure hypothesis:** Joint gaps reveal construction unintentionally. **Check or fix:** Increase overlap or redesign pivot placement. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-029** Can a viewer distinguish a limp from a rigging error? **Failure hypothesis:** Asymmetry has no narrative or physical context. **Check or fix:** Establish intentional gait characteristics consistently. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-030** Are resting hands posed rather than left in a default spread? **Failure hypothesis:** Default rig shape undermines natural acting. **Check or fix:** Pose inactive hands for the character's state. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-031** Does the hero's outline remain distinct in the crowd version? **Failure hypothesis:** Small-scale duplicates lose signature features. **Check or fix:** Create a simplified distance silhouette. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-007-032** Would reducing internal detail strengthen the acting silhouette? **Failure hypothesis:** Decorative strokes interfere with pose reading. **Check or fix:** Compare a simplified contour-first variant. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D008 acting and expression

- **AVQ-008-001** Does the character visibly think before changing strategy? **Failure hypothesis:** Decision appears as arbitrary motion. **Check or fix:** Add a readable attention or realization beat. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-002** Is the reaction proportionate to the event's importance? **Failure hypothesis:** Overacting flattens the emotional hierarchy. **Check or fix:** Reserve the strongest pose for the strongest change. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-003** Do the eyes lead the body toward a new target? **Failure hypothesis:** Movement starts without apparent intention. **Check or fix:** Stage gaze before the directional turn. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-004** Does anticipation reveal the action without spoiling the joke? **Failure hypothesis:** Preparation gives away the entire payoff. **Check or fix:** Tune anticipation to the required surprise. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-005** Are facial expression and body posture telling the same emotion? **Failure hypothesis:** Face and body send conflicting signals unintentionally. **Check or fix:** Compare expression with the full-body pose. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-006** Does the actor acknowledge a failed attempt before trying again? **Failure hypothesis:** Escalation lacks a meaningful consequence. **Check or fix:** Insert a concise reassessment gesture. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-007** Are repeated reactions varied for changing stakes? **Failure hypothesis:** Identical expression cycles feel mechanical. **Check or fix:** Change timing and pose to reflect each outcome. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-008** Can the audience read the emotional change without dialogue? **Failure hypothesis:** Narration carries acting the image fails to show. **Check or fix:** Watch the beat muted with captions hidden. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-009** Does the character look at the actual object being addressed? **Failure hypothesis:** Eyeline misses the interaction target. **Check or fix:** Check gaze vectors against target position. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-010** Is the held expression alive without constant distracting motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Idle animation replaces believable stillness. **Check or fix:** Use restrained breathing or eye activity only when useful. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-011** Does the mouth shape match the intended nonverbal emotion? **Failure hypothesis:** Smile or frown contradicts the scene. **Check or fix:** Inspect facial pose at the reaction peak. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-012** Are blinks placed where they support thought and rhythm? **Failure hypothesis:** Random blinks interrupt key eye contact. **Check or fix:** Retarget blinks around attention shifts. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-013** Does a smile arrive after the character understands the outcome? **Failure hypothesis:** Emotion precedes its cause. **Check or fix:** Align reaction onset with available information. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-014** Can viewers distinguish confidence from confusion? **Failure hypothesis:** Pose vocabulary is ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Compare brows, gaze, torso and timing together. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-015** Does the actor recover physically after a strong action? **Failure hypothesis:** Performance snaps straight back to neutral. **Check or fix:** Add motivated settle and recovery. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-016** Is embarrassment shown without merely shrinking the character? **Failure hypothesis:** Scale change substitutes for acting. **Check or fix:** Use gaze avoidance, posture and timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-017** Do supporting characters have independent reactions? **Failure hypothesis:** Synchronized identical acting looks copied. **Check or fix:** Vary reactions around a shared causal event. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-018** Does the hero's personality shape how the task is attempted? **Failure hypothesis:** Actions could belong to any generic character. **Check or fix:** Choose a characteristic approach or hesitation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-019** Is a silent pause visibly motivated by thought? **Failure hypothesis:** Stillness reads as a frozen render. **Check or fix:** Maintain a clear attention target and poised body. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-020** Does the performance distinguish effort from speed? **Failure hypothesis:** Fast motion alone is used to signal struggle. **Check or fix:** Add resistance, bracing and recovery cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-021** Are emotional transitions given enough intermediate poses? **Failure hypothesis:** Expression pops between incompatible states. **Check or fix:** Add the smallest meaningful transition. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-022** Does the final reaction acknowledge the changed situation? **Failure hypothesis:** Ending pose repeats the opening emotion. **Check or fix:** Show the character's new understanding. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-023** Are gestures directed toward a specific listener or object? **Failure hypothesis:** Arms wave without communicative purpose. **Check or fix:** Assign each gesture a target and intent. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-024** Does the character notice an approaching threat at a plausible time? **Failure hypothesis:** Reaction uses information outside their viewpoint. **Check or fix:** Check sightlines and available cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-025** Is comic resignation distinct from visual lifelessness? **Failure hypothesis:** Underacting removes the joke's emotional payoff. **Check or fix:** Preserve one clear expressive cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-026** Do eyes remain readable while the head turns? **Failure hypothesis:** Expression disappears during the critical transition. **Check or fix:** Adjust turn timing or use a readable angle. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-027** Does surprise propagate through the body rather than only the face? **Failure hypothesis:** Head-only reaction feels disconnected. **Check or fix:** Coordinate shoulders, torso and stance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-028** Are microgestures visible enough to justify their screen time? **Failure hypothesis:** Subtle acting is below delivery-size perception. **Check or fix:** Enlarge the gesture or shorten its dedicated beat. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-029** Does the actor's attention remain consistent during prop handling? **Failure hypothesis:** Character gazes elsewhere during a delicate action. **Check or fix:** Align attention with the task's difficulty. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-030** Can two characters' relationship be inferred from their responses? **Failure hypothesis:** Interaction lacks reciprocity. **Check or fix:** Stage an offer, acknowledgment and changed behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-031** Does the performance leave room for the viewer's own inference? **Failure hypothesis:** Every feeling is redundantly spelled out. **Check or fix:** Remove one redundant gesture or line and compare. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-008-032** Would a stronger contrasting pose improve the turning point? **Failure hypothesis:** Emotional change is too visually incremental. **Check or fix:** Create a clear before-and-after pose contrast. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D009 camera and projection

- **AVQ-009-001** Does the projection suit the spatial relationship being explained? **Failure hypothesis:** Perspective hides the quantity or connection. **Check or fix:** Compare orthographic and perspective evidence views. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-002** Is the horizon consistent across shots in the same location? **Failure hypothesis:** Camera changes imply a different world orientation. **Check or fix:** Check horizon and vanishing directions together. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-003** Does camera movement reveal information rather than merely add activity? **Failure hypothesis:** Motion consumes attention without benefit. **Check or fix:** State what becomes knowable because of the move. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-004** Are camera cuts crossing the action axis intentionally? **Failure hypothesis:** Screen direction reverses without orientation. **Check or fix:** Map the axis and provide a reestablishing view. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-005** Does the lens distort a character's proportions unintentionally? **Failure hypothesis:** Wide-angle proximity changes identity. **Check or fix:** Test focal length and camera distance together. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-006** Is the camera close enough to show the decisive contact? **Failure hypothesis:** Wide framing makes causality microscopic. **Check or fix:** Insert or hold a closer evidence view. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-007** Does a tracking shot keep the destination understandable? **Failure hypothesis:** Following the hero hides the route ahead. **Check or fix:** Include landmarks or a destination glimpse. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-008** Are near and far objects scaled consistently under orthographic projection? **Failure hypothesis:** Manual scaling falsely implies perspective. **Check or fix:** Audit scale overrides against projection rules. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-009-009** Does camera roll carry meaning worth its disorientation? **Failure hypothesis:** Tilt distracts from stable information. **Check or fix:** Compare with a level horizon. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-010** Does a zoom mimic movement that should create parallax? **Failure hypothesis:** Lens scaling is presented as spatial travel. **Check or fix:** Use actual translation or label the graphic treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-011** Is camera acceleration comfortable at the viewing size? **Failure hypothesis:** Abrupt movement creates unintended discomfort. **Check or fix:** Inspect velocity changes and preview full-screen. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-012** Does the camera settle before text must be read? **Failure hypothesis:** Moving frame makes reading unnecessarily difficult. **Check or fix:** Hold or stabilize during the reading interval. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-013** Are all required objects inside near and far clipping planes? **Failure hypothesis:** Camera configuration removes scene content. **Check or fix:** Inspect bounds at every camera extreme. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-009-014** Does an orbit reveal valid alternate sides of the model? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden geometry fails outside the hero view. **Check or fix:** Inspect the entire used orbit before approval. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-015** Is the apparent up direction stable through camera transitions? **Failure hypothesis:** Orientation shifts without an anchoring cue. **Check or fix:** Preserve a landmark or use a motivated transition. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-016** Does depth compression hide separation between interacting objects? **Failure hypothesis:** Long-lens overlap makes contact ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Adjust angle or focal length for spatial clarity. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-017** Are multiple cameras sampling exactly the same action time? **Failure hypothesis:** Matched views show different states. **Check or fix:** Drive all views from one timestamp. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-018** Does a top-down view preserve enough height information? **Failure hypothesis:** Vertical relationships are flattened away. **Check or fix:** Add side evidence or explicit height cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-019** Is camera shake appropriate to the source of motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform shake feels unmotivated or excessive. **Check or fix:** Tie shake onset and decay to the event. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-020** Does the camera lead or lag the hero for a clear reason? **Failure hypothesis:** Framing feels late or anticipates unseen action. **Check or fix:** Choose intentional follow behavior per beat. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-021** Are perspective lines compatible across composited assets? **Failure hypothesis:** Mixed projections make the set incoherent. **Check or fix:** Align vanishing structure or separate graphic planes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-022** Does the camera path pass through geometry? **Failure hypothesis:** View briefly enters walls or objects. **Check or fix:** Check path clearance and intermediate frames. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-023** Is focus breathing distracting during a focus handoff? **Failure hypothesis:** Refocusing unexpectedly changes framing. **Check or fix:** Reduce breathing or compensate composition. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-024** Does a push-in end on the information it promised? **Failure hypothesis:** Move lands on empty or irrelevant detail. **Check or fix:** Define the endpoint before animating the camera. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-025** Are camera speed changes synchronized with emotional emphasis? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform movement ignores the dramatic turn. **Check or fix:** Shape the move around the story beat. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-026** Does the final framing leave adequate platform-overlay space? **Failure hypothesis:** UI covers the subject or conclusion. **Check or fix:** Preview verified target-platform overlay regions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-027** Can a viewer reconstruct geography after repeated close shots? **Failure hypothesis:** Coverage loses spatial orientation. **Check or fix:** Return to a clear establishing relationship. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-028** Does a reverse angle preserve object handedness correctly? **Failure hypothesis:** Mirroring changes tools, text or character features. **Check or fix:** Use a real reverse view or audit all mirrors. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-029** Are camera coordinates and object coordinates using the same convention? **Failure hypothesis:** Axis mismatch produces reversed or tilted motion. **Check or fix:** Test a known directional reference. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-030** Does a locked camera remain genuinely stable after compositing? **Failure hypothesis:** Layer drift creates unintended camera wobble. **Check or fix:** Track fixed landmarks in encoded frames. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-031** Is the camera's proximity emotionally appropriate for the moment? **Failure hypothesis:** Distant framing weakens an intimate response. **Check or fix:** Test a closer reaction while retaining context. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-009-032** Would a simpler camera move make the action easier to judge? **Failure hypothesis:** Complex travel conceals animation weaknesses. **Check or fix:** Review a locked-camera alternative. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D010 lighting and shadows

- **AVQ-010-001** Does the key light reveal the important form? **Failure hypothesis:** Lighting hides the feature being explained. **Check or fix:** Inspect the focal object without decorative lights. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-002** Are cast shadows consistent with the apparent light direction? **Failure hypothesis:** Objects seem lit by unrelated worlds. **Check or fix:** Trace shadows back toward plausible sources. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-003** Does the hero's face remain readable through the action? **Failure hypothesis:** Turning moves expression into unhelpful darkness. **Check or fix:** Review face values at orientation extrema. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-004** Are contact shadows close enough to ground objects? **Failure hypothesis:** Detached shadows create floating. **Check or fix:** Inspect contact regions at native resolution. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-005** Does a moving light change all affected layers consistently? **Failure hypothesis:** Only some objects respond to illumination. **Check or fix:** Audit shared light influence across the composite. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-006** Are specular highlights clipping away important detail? **Failure hypothesis:** Bright reflections erase shape or labels. **Check or fix:** Inspect highlight values and reduce intensity or roughness. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-007** Does the background receive more dramatic lighting than the hero? **Failure hypothesis:** Scenery attracts attention at the wrong time. **Check or fix:** Rebalance light contrast by narrative priority. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-008** Are shadows soft at a scale consistent with the light source? **Failure hypothesis:** Tiny source produces implausibly broad penumbrae. **Check or fix:** Match source size and shadow softness deliberately. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-009** Does ambient fill flatten the scene beyond the intended style? **Failure hypothesis:** All surfaces have similar values. **Check or fix:** Restore controlled directional contrast. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-010** Is a rim light motivated or visibly decorative? **Failure hypothesis:** Artificial outline breaks the material illusion. **Check or fix:** Reduce rim strength or establish its source. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-011** Does lighting change continuity across an unbroken action? **Failure hypothesis:** Cut suggests time or location changed. **Check or fix:** Compare light direction and exposure at the edit. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-012** Are translucent materials transmitting plausible color and light? **Failure hypothesis:** Glass or wax behaves like opaque plastic. **Check or fix:** Inspect transmission against the style's material rules. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-013** Does the shadow show a different pose than the character? **Failure hypothesis:** Cached or mismatched shadow reveals inconsistency. **Check or fix:** Compare pose silhouettes at key frames. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-014** Are overhead objects casting shadows onto the right surfaces? **Failure hypothesis:** Projection ignores receiving geometry. **Check or fix:** Review receiver masks or shadow geometry. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-015** Does a dark scene preserve enough information for the task? **Failure hypothesis:** Atmosphere makes required evidence unreadable. **Check or fix:** Raise selective fill rather than global brightness alone. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-016** Is the lighting gradient stable during camera movement? **Failure hypothesis:** Screen-space shading slides over the world. **Check or fix:** Anchor illumination to the intended coordinate space. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-017** Are emissive objects visibly affecting nearby surfaces when needed? **Failure hypothesis:** Glow looks pasted on rather than luminous. **Check or fix:** Add restrained local response or clarify graphic style. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-018** Does bloom conceal small text or contact edges? **Failure hypothesis:** Glow spreads over evidence-bearing boundaries. **Check or fix:** Limit bloom around required information. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-019** Are transparent shadows appropriately different from opaque shadows? **Failure hypothesis:** Glass casts a solid cutout unexpectedly. **Check or fix:** Adjust transmission or label the stylization. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-020** Does ambient occlusion create dirty halos around moving parts? **Failure hypothesis:** AO exaggeration looks like grime or gaps. **Check or fix:** Reduce radius or strength at joints. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-021** Are shadow transitions temporally stable? **Failure hypothesis:** Sampling noise flickers as geometry moves. **Check or fix:** Inspect consecutive encoded frames in shadow regions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-022** Does exposure adaptation pump during a small bright event? **Failure hypothesis:** Automatic exposure changes the whole scene distracting. **Check or fix:** Lock or smooth exposure for the shot. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-023** Is the eye highlight consistent enough to preserve gaze? **Failure hypothesis:** Highlights wander independently of head and light. **Check or fix:** Track highlight placement across expressions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-024** Are light and shadow colors compatible with the palette? **Failure hypothesis:** Unrelated color temperatures fracture the image. **Check or fix:** Choose a coherent warm-cool lighting relationship. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-025** Does a shadow incorrectly imply a dangerous gap or obstacle? **Failure hypothesis:** Shadow geometry miscommunicates the route. **Check or fix:** Inspect shadows separately from solid surfaces. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-026** Can the important shape be read without its texture? **Failure hypothesis:** Lighting relies on painted detail to suggest volume. **Check or fix:** Review a neutral-material lighting pass. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-027** Does a backlit silhouette reveal the intended pose clearly? **Failure hypothesis:** Merged limbs make backlighting unreadable. **Check or fix:** Adjust pose or local background light. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-028** Are reflections of lights stable on deforming surfaces? **Failure hypothesis:** Highlight popping exposes normal errors. **Check or fix:** Inspect normals and roughness through deformation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-029** Does the final grade preserve shadow detail after encoding? **Failure hypothesis:** Compression crushes subtle dark information. **Check or fix:** Compare source and decoded shadow regions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-030** Is the time-of-day cue compatible with the story's timeline? **Failure hypothesis:** Lighting contradicts stated chronology. **Check or fix:** Check sky, shadow direction and narrative timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-031** Does lighting support the reveal by changing attention naturally? **Failure hypothesis:** Reveal arrives in an already cluttered light pattern. **Check or fix:** Reserve contrast for the newly important element. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-010-032** Would fewer light sources produce a more coherent image? **Failure hypothesis:** Complex lighting creates contradictory cues. **Check or fix:** Test a simplified key-fill setup. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D011 color and materials

- **AVQ-011-001** Can key categories be distinguished without hue alone? **Failure hypothesis:** Color-only coding excludes some viewers. **Check or fix:** Add shape, position or label redundancy. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-002** Do material properties match the action's implied friction? **Failure hypothesis:** Slippery-looking objects behave as high-grip surfaces. **Check or fix:** Align appearance and motion cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-003** Is the accent color reserved for meaningful emphasis? **Failure hypothesis:** Every object uses the attention color. **Check or fix:** Reduce accents on nonessential elements. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-004** Do adjacent materials separate at delivery resolution? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar values merge distinct surfaces. **Check or fix:** Adjust value, roughness or boundary treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-005** Does the material imply a weight the animation contradicts? **Failure hypothesis:** Metal-looking prop moves like foam. **Check or fix:** Revise motion weight or material appearance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-006** Are skin or character colors stable across shots? **Failure hypothesis:** Grading changes identity unintentionally. **Check or fix:** Compare color references under matched illumination. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-007** Is transparency revealing the intended contents clearly? **Failure hypothesis:** Tint and reflections obscure the explanation. **Check or fix:** Tune opacity and reflection balance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-008** Does metallic shading retain shape in the chosen environment? **Failure hypothesis:** Uninformative reflections make metal look flat. **Check or fix:** Provide meaningful reflected structure. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-011-009** Are roughness changes motivated by actual surface variation? **Failure hypothesis:** Random roughness looks like damage. **Check or fix:** Use a coherent material variation map. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-010** Does the palette support the emotional transition? **Failure hypothesis:** Color stays emotionally static despite a major turn. **Check or fix:** Test a restrained motivated palette shift. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-011** Are warning colors used consistently? **Failure hypothesis:** Red or amber sometimes means decoration. **Check or fix:** Audit semantic color assignments. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-012** Does a color gradient accidentally imply quantitative data? **Failure hypothesis:** Decorative shading looks like a heat map. **Check or fix:** Separate decoration from measured encodings. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-013** Are bright saturated regions clipping in the output gamut? **Failure hypothesis:** Color detail collapses after conversion. **Check or fix:** Inspect encoded channels and gamut handling. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-014** Does a white object retain form on a light background? **Failure hypothesis:** High-key palette loses boundaries. **Check or fix:** Use controlled value and shadow separation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-015** Are cloth, paper and clay differentiated by appropriate cues? **Failure hypothesis:** Different materials share generic plastic shading. **Check or fix:** Choose specific roughness, edge and deformation behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-016** Does the material scale match the object's physical scale? **Failure hypothesis:** Grain makes miniature objects look enormous. **Check or fix:** Compare texture feature size with object dimensions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-017** Is the palette coherent across imported and original assets? **Failure hypothesis:** Assets carry incompatible color treatments. **Check or fix:** Normalize them within one approved palette. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-018** Do transparent overlays preserve readability underneath? **Failure hypothesis:** Tint layers reduce contrast unpredictably. **Check or fix:** Test the busiest underlying frame. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-019** Does wetness or gloss appear only where justified? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform shine makes every surface look wet. **Check or fix:** Restrict specularity to material logic. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-020** Are color changes signaling state rather than arbitrary variation? **Failure hypothesis:** Viewers infer a state transition that did not occur. **Check or fix:** Document and audit state-color rules. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-021** Does the final grade preserve the original hierarchy? **Failure hypothesis:** Global treatment promotes previously quiet colors. **Check or fix:** Recheck focal contrast after grading. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-022** Is the background color compatible with the caption treatment? **Failure hypothesis:** Changing scenery makes text contrast unstable. **Check or fix:** Use a stable backing or adaptive verified treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-023** Are textures and base colors fighting each other? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface pattern destroys the intended palette. **Check or fix:** Reduce texture contrast or recolor it coherently. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-024** Does a material's translucency change incorrectly with camera angle? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface appears to pop between solid and transparent. **Check or fix:** Inspect angle-dependent shading behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-025** Are symbolic colors culturally appropriate for the intended audience? **Failure hypothesis:** Color meaning is assumed to be universal. **Check or fix:** Check audience interpretation before relying on symbolism. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-026** Does color distinguish before-and-after states clearly? **Failure hypothesis:** The transformation is too subtle to notice. **Check or fix:** Choose a visible state contrast with redundant cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-027** Are object interiors and cut surfaces materially plausible? **Failure hypothesis:** Cutaway reveals the wrong material identity. **Check or fix:** Assign coherent interior materials. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-028** Does a stylized material remain consistent through deformation? **Failure hypothesis:** Texture or highlights detach from the body. **Check or fix:** Track material coordinates on moving surfaces. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-029** Are dark outlines changing the perceived color category? **Failure hypothesis:** Heavy contour overwhelms small colored regions. **Check or fix:** Review filled shapes at actual display size. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-030** Does the palette accommodate both calm and urgent beats? **Failure hypothesis:** Only one intensity level is available. **Check or fix:** Reserve saturation and contrast for escalation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-031** Can the audience tell a material change from a lighting change? **Failure hypothesis:** Visual cause of transformation is ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Keep one variable stable during the demonstration. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-011-032** Would a smaller material vocabulary improve coherence? **Failure hypothesis:** Too many surface types fragment the world. **Check or fix:** Consolidate materials by narrative function. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D012 texture and detail

- **AVQ-012-001** Does texture obscure the smallest important feature? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface detail competes with evidence. **Check or fix:** Inspect the feature after downsampling and encoding. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-002** Are texture seams visible during the actual camera move? **Failure hypothesis:** UV boundaries become exposed in motion. **Check or fix:** Review all used angles and deformation states. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-003** Does texture swim across moving geometry? **Failure hypothesis:** Coordinates are anchored to the wrong space. **Check or fix:** Track a recognizable texel against the surface. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-004** Are repeating patterns conspicuous enough to break scale? **Failure hypothesis:** Tiling reveals asset construction. **Check or fix:** Vary placement or use a less periodic pattern. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-005** Does fine detail shimmer between frames? **Failure hypothesis:** Subpixel frequencies alias during movement. **Check or fix:** Reduce frequency or improve filtering. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-006** Are distant textures simplified appropriately? **Failure hypothesis:** High-frequency detail creates background noise. **Check or fix:** Use distance-aware detail reduction. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-007** Does grain belong to the image or incorrectly follow objects? **Failure hypothesis:** Noise behavior contradicts the intended medium. **Check or fix:** Choose screen-space or surface-space grain deliberately. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-008** Are texture features consistent with the claimed material? **Failure hypothesis:** Stone grain appears on paper or fabric. **Check or fix:** Use material-specific reference logic. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-009** Does procedural variation produce target-like false positives? **Failure hypothesis:** Random shapes resemble puzzle objects. **Check or fix:** Inspect generated backgrounds before placing targets. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-012-010** Are scratches and wear located where use would cause them? **Failure hypothesis:** Random distressing feels decorative and implausible. **Check or fix:** Place wear around contact and exposure areas. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-011** Is outline detail stable at the output pixel grid? **Failure hypothesis:** Thin strokes flicker after rasterization. **Check or fix:** Test minimum stroke thickness in the final encode. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-012** Does detail density change abruptly between adjacent assets? **Failure hypothesis:** One object looks imported from another production. **Check or fix:** Normalize detail scale and contrast. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-013** Are facial textures preserving expression rather than masking it? **Failure hypothesis:** Patterning obscures eyes and mouth. **Check or fix:** Quiet detail around expressive features. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-014** Does motion blur turn detailed surfaces into distracting streaks? **Failure hypothesis:** Texture dominates blur direction. **Check or fix:** Reduce detail or adjust blur for readability. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-015** Are normal maps creating impossible bumps at silhouettes? **Failure hypothesis:** Shading suggests geometry the outline denies. **Check or fix:** Check silhouette and bump scale together. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-012-016** Does texture remain aligned across a cut or replacement asset? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface identity changes between views. **Check or fix:** Compare persistent landmarks on the material. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-017** Is decorative text baked into a texture actually readable and correct? **Failure hypothesis:** Pseudo-lettering looks like misinformation. **Check or fix:** Replace illegible text with verified lettering or remove it. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-018** Are compression blocks strongest in the most important region? **Failure hypothesis:** Detail consumes bitrate needed for the subject. **Check or fix:** Simplify noisy areas and test encoding allocation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-019** Does a handmade texture repeat too perfectly? **Failure hypothesis:** Mechanical repetition contradicts the intended craft feel. **Check or fix:** Introduce bounded meaningful variation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-020** Are particles sized consistently with the scene scale? **Failure hypothesis:** Dust or fibers imply the wrong object size. **Check or fix:** Compare particle dimensions with familiar references. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-021** Does detail communicate function rather than merely busyness? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface complexity adds no useful information. **Check or fix:** Remove details that neither explain nor characterize. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-022** Are rough edges deliberate at all scales? **Failure hypothesis:** Accidental raster artifacts mimic a broken contour. **Check or fix:** Inspect vector source and encoded edge behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-023** Does a texture atlas leak neighboring colors at borders? **Failure hypothesis:** Sampling creates unwanted edge stripes. **Check or fix:** Check padding and filtering around UV islands. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-024** Are surface markings distorted by deformation unintentionally? **Failure hypothesis:** Labels stretch until misleading. **Check or fix:** Use appropriate attachment or limit deformation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-025** Does a small pattern create moire in the target viewing setup? **Failure hypothesis:** Repeated lines interfere with the display grid. **Check or fix:** Preview the intended downscaled output. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-026** Are texture transitions gradual enough across level-of-detail changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Visible popping interrupts motion. **Check or fix:** Match transition frequency and blend behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-027** Does a procedural seed change between frames unexpectedly? **Failure hypothesis:** Random detail flickers without physical cause. **Check or fix:** Fix seeds or evolve noise continuously. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-028** Are environment textures richer than the hero for a reason? **Failure hypothesis:** Background detail steals attention. **Check or fix:** Reduce environmental detail near the focal action. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-029** Can the scene still be read when texture is temporarily removed? **Failure hypothesis:** Texture compensates for weak structure. **Check or fix:** Review a flat-color structural pass. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-030** Does an important printed mark stay legible on curved surfaces? **Failure hypothesis:** Curvature hides or distorts the identifier. **Check or fix:** Place the mark on a readable region. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-031** Are apparent dirt or damage marks consistent with the story state? **Failure hypothesis:** Wear changes without an event. **Check or fix:** Track material-state continuity. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-012-032** Would larger simpler texture features survive delivery better? **Failure hypothesis:** Microdetail vanishes while retaining visual noise. **Check or fix:** Test a lower-frequency texture variant. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D013 motion and easing

- **AVQ-013-001** Does the action accelerate for a physical or expressive reason? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform motion feels mechanical. **Check or fix:** Shape speed around intention and resistance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-002** Are holds truly still where the pose should be planted? **Failure hypothesis:** Residual interpolation creates unwanted drift. **Check or fix:** Inspect held channels and encoded landmarks. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-003** Does easing delay the action until the beat has passed? **Failure hypothesis:** Overlong ease-in weakens timing. **Check or fix:** Compare onset with the narrative cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-004** Is the motion arc smooth through intermediate poses? **Failure hypothesis:** Keyframe interpolation creates corners. **Check or fix:** Plot and inspect the path between keys. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-005** Do different body parts lead and follow coherently? **Failure hypothesis:** All parts start together like a rigid cutout. **Check or fix:** Stagger initiation and follow-through. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-006** Does overshoot fit the material and emotional tone? **Failure hypothesis:** Elastic easing makes rigid objects rubbery. **Check or fix:** Tune overshoot by object and intent. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-007** Are direction reversals prepared rather than instantaneous? **Failure hypothesis:** Velocity flips without anticipation. **Check or fix:** Add braking, weight shift or stylized punctuation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-008** Does a repeated cycle visibly snap at its seam? **Failure hypothesis:** First and last poses or velocities disagree. **Check or fix:** Compare seam position and derivatives. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-009** Are walk cycles matched to actual travel speed? **Failure hypothesis:** Stride frequency causes skating. **Check or fix:** Relate stride distance to root displacement. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-010** Does motion remain readable after temporal sampling? **Failure hypothesis:** Fast gesture disappears between delivered frames. **Check or fix:** Adjust duration or pose emphasis at output frame rate. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-011** Are interpolation handles creating hidden extreme poses? **Failure hypothesis:** Spline overshoot produces unintended excursions. **Check or fix:** Inspect curves and sampled extrema. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-012** Does a settle end with a clear stable pose? **Failure hypothesis:** Endless damping prevents the beat from landing. **Check or fix:** Choose a meaningful final hold. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-013** Is anticipation proportionate to the upcoming action? **Failure hypothesis:** Large preparation promises an underwhelming result. **Check or fix:** Balance preparation and payoff amplitude. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-014** Are motion trails consistent with trajectory and timing? **Failure hypothesis:** Trails suggest an incorrect movement path. **Check or fix:** Derive trails from actual sampled motion. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-015** Does secondary motion stop before it becomes distracting? **Failure hypothesis:** Hair or scarf continues competing with the next beat. **Check or fix:** Control decay around attention shifts. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-016** Are camera and object easing accidentally canceling each other? **Failure hypothesis:** Combined motion makes the subject appear stalled. **Check or fix:** Inspect relative screen-space velocity. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-017** Does a crowd cycle repeat in obvious synchronized bands? **Failure hypothesis:** Shared phase exposes duplication. **Check or fix:** Vary phase while preserving causal coordination. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-018** Is a slow action slow because it is meaningful? **Failure hypothesis:** Unmotivated slowness wastes the viewer's attention. **Check or fix:** Shorten or enrich the information-bearing interval. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-019** Do moving labels remain attached without jitter? **Failure hypothesis:** Annotation smoothing lags or oscillates. **Check or fix:** Use stable anchors and inspect final projection. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-020** Are speed changes legible without relying on motion blur? **Failure hypothesis:** Blur hides the actual acceleration. **Check or fix:** Review a no-blur motion pass. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-021** Does a character stop with a plausible transfer of weight? **Failure hypothesis:** Root halts while limbs continue incorrectly. **Check or fix:** Coordinate braking steps and torso settle. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-022** Are path corners rounded enough for the object's turning ability? **Failure hypothesis:** Motion cuts through corners or rotates unnaturally. **Check or fix:** Match turn radius to body and environment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.
