- **AVQ-013-023** Does the motion preserve screen direction across edits? **Failure hypothesis:** Action reverses unintentionally at the cut. **Check or fix:** Compare outgoing and incoming velocity vectors. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-024** Are tiny idle loops visible as repetitive nervousness? **Failure hypothesis:** Subtle cycles become distracting over long holds. **Check or fix:** Reduce frequency or use noncyclic pauses. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-025** Does a scale animation imply intentional deformation rather than camera error? **Failure hypothesis:** Object growth looks like accidental zoom. **Check or fix:** Pair deformation with appropriate contour and timing cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-026** Are separate layers driven by one time authority? **Failure hypothesis:** Animation drifts because clocks differ. **Check or fix:** Sample all layers from the same timeline. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-027** Does a delayed reaction still feel causally connected? **Failure hypothesis:** Lag becomes too long to read as response. **Check or fix:** Test the cue-to-reaction interval in context. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-028** Is the movement envelope compatible with safe areas? **Failure hypothesis:** Easing overshoot pushes important content offscreen. **Check or fix:** Check extrema rather than only key positions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-029** Do repeated actions escalate meaningfully in timing or strategy? **Failure hypothesis:** Cycles repeat without advancing the story. **Check or fix:** Change the consequential variable per attempt. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-030** Are contact constraints preserved through interpolation? **Failure hypothesis:** Valid keys hide invalid in-between contacts. **Check or fix:** Sample the full interval at delivery rate or finer. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-031** Does stylized stepped motion use a consistent cadence? **Failure hypothesis:** Uneven holds resemble dropped frames. **Check or fix:** Document and inspect intended pose exposure timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-013-032** Would fewer moving elements make the main action clearer? **Failure hypothesis:** Continuous animation overloads attention. **Check or fix:** Mute secondary motion and compare comprehension. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D014 physics and weight

- **AVQ-014-001** Does the center of mass stay supported during a static pose? **Failure hypothesis:** Body appears to balance on empty space. **Check or fix:** Compare mass projection with the support region. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-002** Is momentum preserved through release and free flight? **Failure hypothesis:** Object changes speed without an apparent force. **Check or fix:** Inspect velocity before and after release. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-003** Does gravity act consistently within the fictional world? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar objects fall under different unexplained rules. **Check or fix:** Compare fall trajectories under matched conditions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-004** Are collisions resolved at the visible surfaces? **Failure hypothesis:** Objects react before touching or after interpenetrating. **Check or fix:** Check contact geometry and reaction frame. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-005** Does an impact produce an appropriate response in both participants? **Failure hypothesis:** Only one object acknowledges the collision. **Check or fix:** Add coherent recoil or justified immovability. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-006** Are heavy objects harder to start and stop than light ones? **Failure hypothesis:** Weight cues contradict material identity. **Check or fix:** Compare acceleration and recovery timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-007** Does a pushed object remain in contact with the pushing hand? **Failure hypothesis:** Force appears transmitted across a gap. **Check or fix:** Track hand-surface distance throughout the push. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-008** Are friction changes motivated by surface changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Objects suddenly stick or slide without cause. **Check or fix:** Audit surface states along the trajectory. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-009** Does a bouncing object lose or gain energy intentionally? **Failure hypothesis:** Bounce heights imply unexplained energy creation. **Check or fix:** Compare successive apex heights and timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-010** Are pendulum or hanging motions constrained to their support? **Failure hypothesis:** Suspended objects travel outside their allowed arc. **Check or fix:** Check pivot distance over time. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-011** Does a flexible object respond where force is applied? **Failure hypothesis:** Deformation ignores load direction. **Check or fix:** Align bend and stretch with force cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-012** Are stacked objects stable under the depicted load? **Failure hypothesis:** Top-heavy arrangements defy the intended realism. **Check or fix:** Inspect support and center-of-mass relationships. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-013** Does water contact match the hull or floating body? **Failure hypothesis:** Object hovers over or sinks through the surface. **Check or fix:** Compare waterline and object geometry. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-014-014** Is a floating object's buoyancy consistent as weight changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Added load has no plausible displacement effect. **Check or fix:** Show or justify the response to loading. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, educational.

- **AVQ-014-015** Are falling objects occluded correctly by intervening geometry? **Failure hypothesis:** Trajectory visually passes through solid structures. **Check or fix:** Check swept volumes and depth order together. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-016** Does a turning character lean coherently with speed? **Failure hypothesis:** Body lean contradicts the turn. **Check or fix:** Compare direction, acceleration and pose. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-017** Are feet slipping only when the scene intends a slip? **Failure hypothesis:** Uncontrolled skating undermines weight. **Check or fix:** Track support-phase contacts separately from swing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-018** Does a rope or cable transmit tension visibly? **Failure hypothesis:** Slack cable pulls an object impossibly. **Check or fix:** Align tension state with the applied action. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-019** Are force effects proportional enough to communicate cause? **Failure hypothesis:** Tiny taps launch huge objects without established stylization. **Check or fix:** Establish exaggeration rules or rebalance response. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-020** Does a projectile clear the obstacle it visibly crosses? **Failure hypothesis:** Trajectory intersects geometry between sampled frames. **Check or fix:** Test the swept path at finer intervals. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-021** Are particles inheriting appropriate motion at emission? **Failure hypothesis:** Debris launches without the source's velocity. **Check or fix:** Include source motion in initial conditions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-022** Does a rotating object maintain a coherent pivot or axle? **Failure hypothesis:** Rotation axis wanders without a joint. **Check or fix:** Track pivot position and orientation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-023** Are gears or wheels synchronized with their visible engagement? **Failure hypothesis:** Meshing objects rotate incompatibly. **Check or fix:** Compare tooth counts, direction and angular rates. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-024** Does a moving platform carry its passenger consistently? **Failure hypothesis:** Passenger drifts relative to the support. **Check or fix:** Track local coordinates during the ride. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-025** Are spring-like motions damped in a plausible direction? **Failure hypothesis:** Oscillation grows unexpectedly or never resolves. **Check or fix:** Inspect amplitude envelope and intended energy input. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-026** Does a character's jump include a readable takeoff and landing? **Failure hypothesis:** Flight starts or ends without force transfer. **Check or fix:** Stage compression, extension and recovery. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-027** Are collision margins appropriate to visible shape rather than proxy size? **Failure hypothesis:** Invisible collision boxes stop objects too early. **Check or fix:** Compare proxy and rendered contact boundaries. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-028** Does an offscreen force have enough setup to be understood? **Failure hypothesis:** Unexplained motion looks like a simulation error. **Check or fix:** Show or establish the force source. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-029** Are physical simplifications disclosed in an educational mechanism? **Failure hypothesis:** Toy behavior is presented as a universal law. **Check or fix:** State the model's assumptions and limits. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-030** Does the animation distinguish kinematic proof from simulation evidence? **Failure hypothesis:** Authored paths are mislabeled as physical validation. **Check or fix:** Record which relationships are tested versus simulated. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-031** Are numerical tolerances small enough for the final camera view? **Failure hypothesis:** World-space error becomes visible after zoom. **Check or fix:** Convert tolerances to projected pixel error. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-014-032** Would one extra weight cue make the interaction convincing? **Failure hypothesis:** Correct trajectories still lack perceived effort. **Check or fix:** Add a clear brace, compression or follow-through. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D015 layers and compositing

- **AVQ-015-001** Are all layers transformed by the intended shared camera? **Failure hypothesis:** Foreground and background drift independently. **Check or fix:** Compare common landmarks across layer transforms. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-002** Do mattes preserve fine features without fringing? **Failure hypothesis:** Hair or thin limbs are eroded or haloed. **Check or fix:** Inspect alpha edges against light and dark backgrounds. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-003** Is premultiplied alpha handled consistently? **Failure hypothesis:** Dark or bright fringes appear at transparency edges. **Check or fix:** Verify alpha convention at each compositing boundary. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-004** Are blend modes appropriate for the intended physical effect? **Failure hypothesis:** Additive or multiply treatment distorts object identity. **Check or fix:** Compare isolated and composite layer appearance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-005** Does the layer stack reflect causal depth rather than file order? **Failure hypothesis:** New assets appear above everything accidentally. **Check or fix:** Use explicit semantic depth ordering. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-006** Are shadows composited once rather than duplicated? **Failure hypothesis:** Repeated shadow layers overdarken contact regions. **Check or fix:** Audit shadow contribution per object. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-007** Does global grading apply consistently to all intended layers? **Failure hypothesis:** Overlays and scene assets have mismatched treatment. **Check or fix:** Document which layers bypass the grade and why. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-008** Are masks animated in the same coordinate space as their targets? **Failure hypothesis:** Mask lag exposes hidden content. **Check or fix:** Track mask-target alignment through the move. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-009** Does a replacement background preserve existing light and perspective cues? **Failure hypothesis:** Swap breaks integration while retaining old foreground. **Check or fix:** Recheck projection, exposure and shadow compatibility. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-010** Are motion-blurred edges compatible across adjacent layers? **Failure hypothesis:** Sharp cutouts sit against smeared scenery. **Check or fix:** Match shutter assumptions or use deliberate graphic separation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-011** Do overlays remain crisp without overpowering the scene? **Failure hypothesis:** UI sharpness makes the world look accidentally soft. **Check or fix:** Balance overlay contrast and scene detail. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-012** Are cached layers invalidated after dependent changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Final composite includes stale animation or lighting. **Check or fix:** Record dependency hashes and refresh affected caches. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-013** Does a hidden layer still cast an unintended effect? **Failure hypothesis:** Invisible object leaves a shadow, glow or reflection. **Check or fix:** Audit secondary contributions of disabled elements. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-014** Are nested transforms applied exactly once? **Failure hypothesis:** Parent and child scaling double the movement. **Check or fix:** Test a known anchor through the transform chain. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-015** Does a screen-space effect remain confined to its intended region? **Failure hypothesis:** Glow, blur or displacement leaks into captions. **Check or fix:** Inspect effect masks around protected areas. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-016** Are depth mattes temporally aligned with color frames? **Failure hypothesis:** One-frame offset creates edge tearing. **Check or fix:** Compare frame indices across render passes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-017** Does a color key remove similarly colored subject details? **Failure hypothesis:** Keying erases clothing or props. **Check or fix:** Review retained subject pixels against the key range. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-018** Are transparent assets stored with enough edge color information? **Failure hypothesis:** Resampling reveals contaminated matte colors. **Check or fix:** Inspect edge pixels before and after scaling. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-019** Does compositing preserve the intended color space? **Failure hypothesis:** Linear and display-referred operations are mixed. **Check or fix:** Document conversions around blends and grading. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-020** Are repeated elements independently addressable when the story requires it? **Failure hypothesis:** One baked layer prevents necessary repairs. **Check or fix:** Separate only the elements needing distinct control. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-021** Does a foreground wipe reveal the correct underlying shot at every frame? **Failure hypothesis:** Transition matte exposes stale or wrong content. **Check or fix:** Inspect the wipe boundary across its full duration. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-022** Are adjustment layers scoped to the correct shot range? **Failure hypothesis:** Grade or blur leaks into neighboring scenes. **Check or fix:** Check in/out boundaries and nested compositions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-023** Does a drop shadow imply inappropriate physical depth for text? **Failure hypothesis:** Labels look like floating solid objects. **Check or fix:** Use annotation-specific shadow restraint. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-024** Are compound masks preserving holes and internal contours? **Failure hypothesis:** Fill rules close intended openings. **Check or fix:** Inspect even-odd and winding behavior on key shapes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-025** Does resampling order unnecessarily soften the subject? **Failure hypothesis:** Repeated scale operations degrade edges. **Check or fix:** Composite at an appropriate master scale once. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-026** Are layer names and IDs sufficient to locate the visible defect? **Failure hypothesis:** Opaque naming makes targeted repair expensive. **Check or fix:** Map visible elements to stable source identifiers. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-027** Does the composite include unintended guide or debug layers? **Failure hypothesis:** Production scaffolding appears in the final movie. **Check or fix:** Scan final frames for guides, handles and diagnostics. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-028** Are reflections derived from the same current scene state? **Failure hypothesis:** Reflection layer contains outdated poses. **Check or fix:** Link reflection generation to the shared state. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-029** Does an effect respect occlusion by nearer objects? **Failure hypothesis:** Glow or particles appear through solid foregrounds. **Check or fix:** Apply appropriate depth-aware masks. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-030** Are per-layer frame rates reconciled explicitly? **Failure hypothesis:** Different rates create stutter or duplicated motion. **Check or fix:** Normalize timing while preserving source intent. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-031** Does a compositing shortcut remain valid at the largest planned camera move? **Failure hypothesis:** Patch works only in the preview framing. **Check or fix:** Stress-test the actual camera envelope. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-015-032** Would separating one problematic layer enable a much cleaner fix? **Failure hypothesis:** Baked composite prevents precise correction. **Check or fix:** Isolate the smallest repairable element. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D016 continuity and state

- **AVQ-016-001** Does the important object keep the same owner across the cut? **Failure hypothesis:** Possession changes without an exchange. **Check or fix:** Compare ownership immediately before and after edits. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-002** Are character screen directions consistent through continuous travel? **Failure hypothesis:** Cut reverses the apparent route. **Check or fix:** Track world path and projected direction. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-003** Does the costume retain meaningful details between shots? **Failure hypothesis:** Character identity changes unintentionally. **Check or fix:** Compare distinctive features across adjacent views. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-004** Are persistent props present in the correct locations after reframing? **Failure hypothesis:** Set objects vanish or teleport. **Check or fix:** Use a scene-state ledger and frame comparison. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-005** Does elapsed story time match visible environmental change? **Failure hypothesis:** Lighting or wear changes too quickly. **Check or fix:** Audit chronology against state changes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-006** Are actions matched at the same phase across an edit? **Failure hypothesis:** Motion jumps backward or repeats. **Check or fix:** Compare pose and velocity around the cut. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-007** Does a consumed or broken object remain changed afterward? **Failure hypothesis:** State resets undermine consequence. **Check or fix:** Track irreversible state transitions explicitly. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-008** Are crowds preserving entity identity through occlusion and return? **Failure hypothesis:** Duplicate or missing actors alter the story. **Check or fix:** Audit persistent IDs rather than visible count alone. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-009** Does the timeline preserve the order of cause and effect? **Failure hypothesis:** Result appears before its enabling event. **Check or fix:** Check event dependencies against frame times. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-010** Are labels describing the current state rather than the previous shot? **Failure hypothesis:** Overlay update lags the scene. **Check or fix:** Join label state to the same event clock. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-011** Does a map remain geometrically consistent across zooms? **Failure hypothesis:** Local views contradict the overview. **Check or fix:** Compare landmarks in shared world coordinates. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-012** Are the hero's handedness and held items stable? **Failure hypothesis:** Mirrored shots swap meaningful hands. **Check or fix:** Audit left-right semantics at each camera change. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-013** Does a door or obstacle remain open after being moved? **Failure hypothesis:** Environment resets without narrative explanation. **Check or fix:** Retain state across scene boundaries. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-014** Are marks, damage and dirt consistent with prior events? **Failure hypothesis:** Evidence of action disappears. **Check or fix:** Track material changes as persistent states. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-015** Does a returning character enter from a plausible location? **Failure hypothesis:** Reentry contradicts the established exit. **Check or fix:** Map offscreen travel and available time. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-016** Are numerical counters updated at the event they claim to count? **Failure hypothesis:** Display increments early or twice. **Check or fix:** Bind counters to unique event IDs. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-017** Does a clock distinguish story time from playback time clearly? **Failure hypothesis:** Timer implies an inaccurate duration. **Check or fix:** Document timer semantics and check displayed values. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-018** Are alternative viewpoints synchronized to the same object state? **Failure hypothesis:** Comparison panels show incompatible moments. **Check or fix:** Use one authoritative state sample per frame. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-019** Does a repeated flashback look distinct from current action? **Failure hypothesis:** Temporal structure is ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Use a consistent bounded flashback cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-020** Are object scale changes motivated by camera or story events? **Failure hypothesis:** Props resize between shots unnoticed by the script. **Check or fix:** Compare world dimensions and projection separately. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-021** Does a loop return to a compatible starting state? **Failure hypothesis:** Ending creates a discontinuity on replay. **Check or fix:** Inspect seam pose, ownership and environment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-022** Are hidden objects still accounted for in the state model? **Failure hypothesis:** Occlusion is mistaken for deletion. **Check or fix:** Separate visibility from existence. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-016-023** Does the final count refer to entities rather than repeated appearances? **Failure hypothesis:** Reentry inflates the claimed population. **Check or fix:** Count unique IDs under the declared rule. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-024** Are scene resets labeled when demonstrating alternatives? **Failure hypothesis:** Viewer interprets a comparison as continuous action. **Check or fix:** Mark resets or maintain a clear split-screen structure. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-025** Does the narrative preserve information characters have learned? **Failure hypothesis:** Actor forgets the solution to prolong the plot. **Check or fix:** Track knowledge state alongside physical state. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-026** Are shadows and reflections consistent with object existence? **Failure hypothesis:** Removed objects leave visual evidence behind. **Check or fix:** Check secondary passes after state changes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-027** Does the final artifact correspond to the cited source revision? **Failure hypothesis:** Review evidence describes an older movie. **Check or fix:** Match hashes and version identifiers before approval. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-028** Are title, captions and narration using the same names and numbers? **Failure hypothesis:** Parallel text sources drift apart. **Check or fix:** Compare against one approved script ledger. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-029** Does a repaired shot retain compatibility with neighboring shots? **Failure hypothesis:** Local fix introduces a new continuity break. **Check or fix:** Review both boundaries after replacement. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-030** Are transition overlaps creating a brief duplicate of a unique object? **Failure hypothesis:** Crossfade looks like two simultaneous copies. **Check or fix:** Inspect mixed frames at peak overlap. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-031** Does the ending retain the unresolved constraint needed for the joke? **Failure hypothesis:** Final state accidentally removes the comic problem. **Check or fix:** Compare payoff requirements with the actual tableau. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-016-032** Would a small continuity cue prevent a major viewer misreading? **Failure hypothesis:** State change is correct but visually unannounced. **Check or fix:** Add one clear possession, location or time cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, machine. **Stages:** storyboard, production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D017 pacing and holds

- **AVQ-017-001** Does the opening hold let a first-time viewer locate the subject before the first important action begins? **Failure hypothesis:** Action starts before visual orientation completes. **Check or fix:** Test an unbriefed first viewing and move the action after orientation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-002** Is the last readable frame of the problem held long enough to inspect every required condition? **Failure hypothesis:** The question disappears before its constraints can be read. **Check or fix:** Measure actual reading completion and extend the fully resolved problem frame. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-017-003** Does the pause before a character's choice occur after the audience has seen what each option costs? **Failure hypothesis:** Suspense precedes information needed to care about the choice. **Check or fix:** Move the pause after the cost-establishing shots. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-017-004** Does a reveal remain visible long enough for viewers to connect it back to the earlier clue? **Failure hypothesis:** The edit leaves before recognition catches up. **Check or fix:** Compare immediate and slightly longer reveal holds with new viewers. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle, fiction.

- **AVQ-017-005** Are repeated demonstration cycles still adding information after the audience understands the pattern? **Failure hypothesis:** Identical cycles consume runtime without advancing understanding. **Check or fix:** Remove one repetition and test whether the inference still holds. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-006** Does the montage accelerate before any individual example becomes too brief to identify? **Failure hypothesis:** Escalating shot speed erases the examples it is meant to summarize. **Check or fix:** Find the shortest reliably identified shot and retime the montage around it. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-007** Can viewers finish looking from a label to the object it names before the label changes? **Failure hypothesis:** Label turnover is faster than the required eye movement. **Check or fix:** Hold each label across a complete label-to-object inspection. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, typographic.

- **AVQ-017-008** Does a quiet reaction beat expose a new emotion rather than merely freezing the same pose? **Failure hypothesis:** A nominal pause provides no evolving performance to watch. **Check or fix:** Add a motivated reaction change or shorten the hold. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-017-009** Is the explanation allowed a processing pause after its hardest inference rather than only between sentences? **Failure hypothesis:** Evenly spaced pauses ignore where cognitive effort peaks. **Check or fix:** Ask viewers to explain each inference and insert space at the bottleneck. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-010** Does the countdown begin only when the complete puzzle is available for inspection? **Failure hypothesis:** Intro animation silently consumes the promised solving time. **Check or fix:** Start the timer at the first fully inspectable puzzle frame. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-017-011** Is the apparent urgency appropriate to the task, especially when correct action requires careful checking? **Failure hypothesis:** Fast cutting rewards hurried decisions in a deliberate procedure. **Check or fix:** Repace the sequence around the actual checking steps. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-012** Does slowing a shot preserve readable movement rather than expose an unintentional freeze or cadence break? **Failure hypothesis:** Excessive retiming makes the intended continuous action look stalled. **Check or fix:** Inspect frame progression and choose an intentional slow-motion treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-013** Can the audience predict the destination before a long camera move finishes, making its remaining travel expendable? **Failure hypothesis:** Camera transit continues after its information has been delivered. **Check or fix:** Trim redundant travel while retaining spatial orientation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-014** Does each new section begin before the previous conclusion has had time to register? **Failure hypothesis:** Back-to-back chapter changes overwrite the preceding takeaway. **Check or fix:** Add a short resolved beat or simplify the next opening. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-015** Does the final message survive a platform loop without being interrupted by the opening? **Failure hypothesis:** Loop restart truncates the audience's last reading or emotional beat. **Check or fix:** Watch repeated playback and extend or restructure the endpoint. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-016** Is the longest static hold placed where viewers have something meaningful to examine? **Failure hypothesis:** Runtime is allocated to an already exhausted image. **Check or fix:** Reassign hold time to a denser evidence or reaction shot. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-017** When two facts arrive together, is there enough time to compare them rather than merely notice both? **Failure hypothesis:** Side-by-side evidence is displayed for recognition time, not comparison time. **Check or fix:** Test the stated comparison question and extend the shared interval. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-018** Does a joke leave space for the audience's laugh without hiding the next essential line? **Failure hypothesis:** The next information beat lands during the likely reaction. **Check or fix:** Listen with viewers and delay or simplify the following line. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-017-019** Is the pacing difference between anticipation, impact, and recovery perceptible at ordinary playback speed? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform timing flattens the action into a mechanical sequence. **Check or fix:** Contrast the three phases in the animatic and retime selectively. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-017-020** Does a duration target force deletion of the only shot that explains a causal step? **Failure hypothesis:** Runtime compliance is achieved by removing necessary reasoning. **Check or fix:** Preserve the causal shot and cut lower-value repetition instead. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, fiction.

- **AVQ-017-021** Do viewers mistake an intentional contemplative hold for a stalled player? **Failure hypothesis:** The held image provides no cue that the film is still progressing. **Check or fix:** Test the hold cold and add subtle meaningful progression if needed. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-022** Does narration continue asking viewers to absorb facts while the image demands intensive visual search? **Failure hypothesis:** Reading, listening, and searching compete for the same interval. **Check or fix:** Separate the search window from new spoken instruction. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-017-023** Are crossfades stealing useful exposure from shots already near the minimum readable duration? **Failure hypothesis:** Most of a short shot is spent in a blended, ambiguous state. **Check or fix:** Measure fully settled exposure and shorten dissolves or extend shots. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-024** Does a build-up repeat the same emotional intensity until the climax has nowhere to go? **Failure hypothesis:** Constant maximum energy removes escalation and contrast. **Check or fix:** Chart perceived intensity and create a lower-energy baseline. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-017-025** Is the live demonstration's waiting time honestly represented when it is compressed? **Failure hypothesis:** A brisk cut makes a slow real-world step look instantaneous. **Check or fix:** Mark elapsed time or retain a representative waiting cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, edit. **Style filters:** educational, live-action, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-017-026** Does the video give the viewer a moment to answer a direct question before answering it itself? **Failure hypothesis:** A rhetorical prompt masquerades as a genuine participation opportunity. **Check or fix:** Time an unbriefed response and delay the answer accordingly. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-017-027** Does the hold on an error last so long that viewers encode it as the recommended behavior? **Failure hypothesis:** Incorrect content receives more emphasis than its correction. **Check or fix:** Label the error immediately and give the correction a clear settled beat. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-017-028** Is a fast action replayed only after viewers understand why they are watching it again? **Failure hypothesis:** Repetition arrives without a new observation goal. **Check or fix:** Introduce the detail to watch before the replay. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-029** Can a viewer who blinks during the decisive event recover its meaning from the surrounding shots? **Failure hypothesis:** Essential information exists in a single unreinforced instant. **Check or fix:** Preserve consequence or add a motivated replay without spoiling surprise. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-030** Does the closing credit or logo hold disproportionately dilute a very short film's useful runtime? **Failure hypothesis:** Branding consumes the resolution interval and weakens the ending. **Check or fix:** Compare completion and recall with a shorter credit treatment. **Severity:** low. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-031** Are beat lengths varied because of content, rather than because every scene was generated from one duration template? **Failure hypothesis:** Template timing forces different cognitive tasks into identical slots. **Check or fix:** Assign duration from each shot's action and reading demand. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-017-032** Does the final export preserve the approved pause lengths after frame-rate conversion or speed conforming? **Failure hypothesis:** A technical conform changes the timing decisions already reviewed. **Check or fix:** Compare landmark timestamps between approved reference and delivered encode. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

### D018 story setup payoff

- **AVQ-018-001** What visible event establishes what the protagonist wants before the first obstacle appears? **Failure hypothesis:** The plot moves before a goal gives its events meaning. **Check or fix:** Add or clarify a goal-defining action in the opening. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-002** Is the obstacle caused by the established situation, or does an unexplained intervention manufacture it? **Failure hypothesis:** Arbitrary trouble replaces causal storytelling. **Check or fix:** Connect the obstacle to a shown condition or earlier choice. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-003** Does the eventual solution use a capability or resource the audience had a fair chance to notice? **Failure hypothesis:** An unseeded resource resolves the conflict without earned setup. **Check or fix:** Seed the resource naturally before its decisive use. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-004** Can viewers tell what failure would cost the character without being told in a long caption? **Failure hypothesis:** Stakes remain abstract and do not support the action. **Check or fix:** Show one concrete consequence of failure before escalation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-005** Does the character's final choice differ meaningfully from the choice they would have made at the start? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface action conceals an unchanged dramatic state. **Check or fix:** Define the changed belief or keep the piece explicitly observational. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-006** Is the payoff answering the specific expectation created by the opening image or line? **Failure hypothesis:** The film promises one question and resolves another. **Check or fix:** Write the promise and payoff side by side and reconcile them. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-018-007** Does an early background detail actually matter later, or merely imitate the look of foreshadowing? **Failure hypothesis:** Decorative setup attracts attention without narrative return. **Check or fix:** Pay off, downweight, or remove the conspicuous detail. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-008** Can viewers distinguish a deliberate misdirection from information the film simply withheld unfairly? **Failure hypothesis:** Surprise depends on hiding facts the chosen viewpoint should reveal. **Check or fix:** Audit viewpoint access and plant an honest alternative interpretation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction, puzzle.

- **AVQ-018-009** Does the protagonist cause the resolution through an understandable action? **Failure hypothesis:** Coincidence completes the story while the protagonist watches. **Check or fix:** Link the resolution to a choice, effort, or purposeful nonaction. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-010** Is the helper's intervention motivated before it becomes essential? **Failure hypothesis:** A convenient helper enters solely to repair the plot. **Check or fix:** Establish the helper's motive or prior relationship. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-011** Do repeated failed attempts change the character's understanding or the audience's expectation? **Failure hypothesis:** Escalation is padded with interchangeable failures. **Check or fix:** Give each attempt a distinct lesson, cost, or strategy change. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-012** Does the middle contain a meaningful change in circumstances rather than a longer version of the opening problem? **Failure hypothesis:** The story stalls between premise and ending. **Check or fix:** Introduce a consequence that forces a different approach. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-013** Is a comic reversal grounded in a recognizable expectation that the audience actually forms? **Failure hypothesis:** The punchline overturns an assumption viewers never made. **Check or fix:** Test predictions immediately before the reversal and adjust setup. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-014** Can the central relationship be inferred from behavior instead of unexplained symbolic labels? **Failure hypothesis:** Relationship stakes depend on exposition the film never supplies. **Check or fix:** Add an interaction that demonstrates the relevant bond or tension. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-015** Does a flashback explain the present action without introducing a second unresolved story? **Failure hypothesis:** Backstory competes with the current goal and creates stray promises. **Check or fix:** Limit the flashback to the fact needed for the present decision. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-016** Is the audience supposed to know more than the character, and is that knowledge difference consistently maintained? **Failure hypothesis:** Inconsistent dramatic irony makes reactions look irrational. **Check or fix:** Track audience and character knowledge at every reveal. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-017** Does the ending show the consequence of the solution, rather than cutting immediately after the successful action? **Failure hypothesis:** Resolution is asserted without a visible changed state. **Check or fix:** Add a concise consequence or reaction that confirms the result. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-018** Is an ambiguous ending intentionally bounded to a small set of interpretations? **Failure hypothesis:** Missing information is mistaken for purposeful ambiguity. **Check or fix:** State the intended open question and test plausible readings. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-019** Does an emotional reversal have a visible trigger that belongs to this character? **Failure hypothesis:** Music or narration substitutes for motivated emotional change. **Check or fix:** Show the triggering observation or decision in performance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-020** Are objects important to the payoff introduced with enough identity to be recognized later? **Failure hypothesis:** The audience sees a prop but cannot connect its later appearance. **Check or fix:** Preserve a distinctive feature and stage its first appearance clearly. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-021** Does the resolution honor the world's established rules about ability, scale, or consequence? **Failure hypothesis:** The film changes its own rules to obtain a convenient ending. **Check or fix:** Compare the payoff against a short explicit world-rule sheet. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-022** Does a supposed sacrifice actually remove something the film has shown to matter? **Failure hypothesis:** The story asks for emotion without demonstrating a real cost. **Check or fix:** Establish the value beforehand and show the loss afterward. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-023** Can a short explanatory story be summarized as a causal chain rather than a list of related facts? **Failure hypothesis:** Topic coherence is mistaken for an explanatory argument. **Check or fix:** Connect each fact to the mechanism the next beat develops. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-018-024** Does the opening question remain answerable from the evidence actually shown? **Failure hypothesis:** The story drifts into a broader claim than its demonstration supports. **Check or fix:** Narrow the question or supply the missing evidence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-018-025** Is the demonstration's result revealed only after the viewer has seen the relevant starting conditions? **Failure hypothesis:** Outcome-first editing makes a causal lesson impossible to evaluate. **Check or fix:** Show the necessary baseline before interpreting the result. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-018-026** Does a moral or takeaway emerge from the shown consequences rather than contradicting them? **Failure hypothesis:** The closing lesson is appended despite contrary story evidence. **Check or fix:** Rewrite the takeaway to match the events or revise those events. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, fiction.

- **AVQ-018-027** Do two parallel storylines intersect for a reason the audience can explain? **Failure hypothesis:** Alternation suggests a relationship that never becomes meaningful. **Check or fix:** Establish the shared cause, contrast, or eventual interaction. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-028** Is a character's misunderstanding plausible from what they actually saw and heard? **Failure hypothesis:** The plot relies on ignorance despite available information. **Check or fix:** Audit the character's sensory access and adjust the mistaken inference. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-029** Does a callback change the meaning of the original beat rather than merely repeat it? **Failure hypothesis:** Familiarity is used as a substitute for a payoff. **Check or fix:** Give the repeated action a new consequence or interpretation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-018-030** Is a cliffhanger appropriate to the promised viewing experience, or does it withhold the only useful answer? **Failure hypothesis:** An incomplete ending breaks the short film's central promise. **Check or fix:** Resolve the promised unit before opening a new question. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-018-031** If the final caption were removed, would the key emotional or causal resolution still be visible? **Failure hypothesis:** Explanatory text conceals an unstaged ending. **Check or fix:** Test a caption-free cut and strengthen the decisive visual action. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, fiction.

- **AVQ-018-032** Could the premise survive a change of palette and rendering style because its dramatic mechanism is specific? **Failure hypothesis:** Cosmetic variation is being counted as a genuinely new story. **Check or fix:** Compare goals, obstacles, choices, and outcomes across related pieces. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** fiction.

### D019 transitions and editing

- **AVQ-019-001** Does the cut preserve the moving subject's screen direction when viewers need to track a continuous journey? **Failure hypothesis:** A direction reversal implies a turn that never happened. **Check or fix:** Insert an orienting shot or choose directionally consistent coverage. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-002** Is a jump across the action axis motivated by a visible crossing or a clear new orientation? **Failure hypothesis:** Unsignaled axis crossing reverses spatial relationships. **Check or fix:** Establish the new axis before relying on left-right meaning. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction, 3d.

- **AVQ-019-003** Does an action match join the same physical phase rather than replaying or skipping the decisive movement? **Failure hypothesis:** The cut duplicates or omits part of one action. **Check or fix:** Align contact and limb landmarks across both takes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-004** Does the viewer's gaze land near the next shot's important subject after the cut? **Failure hypothesis:** A large unprepared attention jump hides the next event. **Check or fix:** Reframe, delay the event, or guide gaze before cutting. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-005** Is a dissolve briefly creating a false composite count, relationship, or impossible object? **Failure hypothesis:** Overlapping shots produce misleading intermediate evidence. **Check or fix:** Inspect transition frames and use a clean cut for exact evidence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-019-006** Does a wipe reveal scene information in a deliberate order rather than cover the clue at the crucial moment? **Failure hypothesis:** Transition geometry dictates an unintended information sequence. **Check or fix:** Align the wipe path with the intended reveal order. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-019-007** Are match cuts based on meaningful shared form or action, rather than a resemblance too weak to read? **Failure hypothesis:** The conceptual connection depends on an unnoticed visual match. **Check or fix:** Test the cut without explanation and strengthen the common anchor. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-008** Does a temporal jump make elapsed time legible without being mistaken for continuous action? **Failure hypothesis:** A cut compresses time while preserving misleading continuity cues. **Check or fix:** Add a changed-state cue or concise elapsed-time marker. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-009** Can viewers identify a location change before interpreting the next action? **Failure hypothesis:** A new setting inherits the previous scene's spatial assumptions. **Check or fix:** Establish one distinctive spatial cue before the action. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-010** Does an inserted reaction shot belong to the event and moment viewers are likely to attribute to it? **Failure hypothesis:** Editorial juxtaposition fabricates a response or relationship. **Check or fix:** Verify take context and use an honestly corresponding reaction. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action.

- **AVQ-019-011** Does a cutaway conceal the only evidence that an operation actually succeeded? **Failure hypothesis:** Editing replaces observable completion with an implied result. **Check or fix:** Retain the critical completion interval or label the discontinuity. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** screen-recording, educational.

- **AVQ-019-012** Are transition effects consistent with the distinction between memory, imagination, and present action? **Failure hypothesis:** The same visual grammar marks incompatible narrative states. **Check or fix:** Assign a readable transition rule to each state. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-019-013** Does the return from an insert reestablish the subject's position after the audience has lost spatial context? **Failure hypothesis:** A close detail interrupts tracking without a way back. **Check or fix:** Return to a matching anchor or include an orienting view. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-014** Is a hidden cut exposed by a mismatch in texture, object position, or movement speed? **Failure hypothesis:** A supposedly seamless join contains a local discontinuity. **Check or fix:** Compare both sides frame by frame and repair or make the cut explicit. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-015** Does a crossfade between faces or text create a distracting hybrid expression or unreadable word? **Failure hypothesis:** Blended semantic features attract attention to the transition itself. **Check or fix:** Shorten the overlap or choose a nonblending transition. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action, typographic, cutout, fiction.

- **AVQ-019-016** Is the shot sequence using eyelines that point toward the intended person or object? **Failure hypothesis:** Mismatched look direction connects the wrong spatial targets. **Check or fix:** Plot eyelines and replace or reposition inconsistent coverage. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction, 3d, cutout.

- **AVQ-019-017** Does the edit accidentally imply that two simultaneous processes happened one after another? **Failure hypothesis:** Sequential presentation changes the timing relationship. **Check or fix:** Add overlap, split-screen, or an explicit simultaneity cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-019-018** Do split-screen panels remain synchronized at meaningful events after separate retiming? **Failure hypothesis:** Independent edits fabricate a comparative advantage or delay. **Check or fix:** Lock shared landmarks to one time reference. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-019-019** Is an L-cut carrying dialogue into a shot where viewers might attribute it to the wrong speaker? **Failure hypothesis:** Continued speech is reassigned by the new image. **Check or fix:** Clarify speaker identity or change the cut point. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction.

- **AVQ-019-020** Does a J-cut reveal a place, person, or answer earlier than the visual story intends? **Failure hypothesis:** Incoming sound spoils a planned visual reveal. **Check or fix:** Check the reveal once without prior knowledge and move the audio entrance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction, puzzle.

- **AVQ-019-021** Do alternating close-ups preserve the number and identity of objects in the shared scene? **Failure hypothesis:** Coverage changes make one object look like several or vice versa. **Check or fix:** Maintain distinctive identifiers and a confirming wide shot. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle, educational.

- **AVQ-019-022** Does cutting on every musical beat flatten the distinction between important actions and decorative motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Beat-grid obedience overrides editorial emphasis. **Check or fix:** Reserve selected cuts for meaningful events and let minor beats pass. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-023** Does a transition rely on motion blur that removes evidence viewers need to verify continuity? **Failure hypothesis:** Visual concealment masks inconsistent scene state. **Check or fix:** Inspect clean boundary frames and repair underlying continuity. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-024** Are freeze-frame inserts clearly intentional rather than accidental dropped motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Unmarked frozen footage resembles playback failure. **Check or fix:** Add an editorial cue or preserve subtle contextual motion. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-025** Does a black-frame punctuation create an unintended apparent end before more essential content follows? **Failure hypothesis:** A long black interval triggers abandonment. **Check or fix:** Shorten the gap or keep an unmistakable continuation cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-026** Is the transition out of a screen recording hiding a click or system response needed to reproduce the task? **Failure hypothesis:** Edit compression removes a necessary interaction. **Check or fix:** Keep the command and resulting state together or annotate the omission. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** screen-recording, educational.

- **AVQ-019-027** Does the edit preserve a physical object's trajectory through an occlusion rather than teleport it to a convenient position? **Failure hypothesis:** Occlusion is used to disguise an impossible path. **Check or fix:** Compare pre/post-occlusion position and plausible travel time. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, live-action, stop-motion, fiction.

- **AVQ-019-028** Are parallel cuts suggesting causation where only coincidence is supported? **Failure hypothesis:** Juxtaposition converts correlation into an apparent causal link. **Check or fix:** Reorder or label the relationship to match the evidence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational, live-action.

- **AVQ-019-029** Does an aspect-ratio change carry meaning or merely distract at a scene boundary? **Failure hypothesis:** Unexplained framing shifts feel like an export or source mismatch. **Check or fix:** Standardize framing or establish the narrative reason for the shift. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-030** Does the final cut remove the last tiny movement required to see an object settle or a gesture finish? **Failure hypothesis:** Action is truncated at nominal contact rather than perceptual completion. **Check or fix:** Include recovery and check the endpoint at normal speed. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-019-031** Are alternate takes introducing continuity errors in prop state, costume, or handedness that affect understanding? **Failure hypothesis:** The edit joins incompatible world states. **Check or fix:** Audit story-relevant state on both sides of every coverage change. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action, stop-motion, fiction.

- **AVQ-019-032** If an elaborate transition is replaced with a straight cut, what comprehension or emotion is actually lost? **Failure hypothesis:** Decorative transitions add workload without serving the story. **Check or fix:** Compare a plain-cut version and retain only purposeful complexity. **Severity:** low. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

### D020 text typography

- **AVQ-020-001** Can the smallest essential label be read at the intended phone viewing size without zooming? **Failure hypothesis:** Native-resolution review hides text that collapses at delivery scale. **Check or fix:** Inspect the encoded video at ordinary phone size and enlarge necessary labels. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-002** Do thin letter strokes survive the final encode rather than flicker or disappear? **Failure hypothesis:** Compression and rasterization erase fine typographic detail. **Check or fix:** Compare source and encoded crops across several frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-003** Is the typeface's distinction between similar characters sufficient for the exact codes, numbers, or names shown? **Failure hypothesis:** Ambiguous glyphs change the information a viewer reads. **Check or fix:** Test the actual strings and replace the face or annotate ambiguous characters. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** screen-recording, educational, typographic.

- **AVQ-020-004** Does text remain readable across the brightest and darkest parts of the moving background? **Failure hypothesis:** A single contrast check misses background changes under the text. **Check or fix:** Inspect the complete text interval and add a stable backing where needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-005** Are line breaks preserving phrases that must be understood together? **Failure hypothesis:** Awkward wrapping splits negation, quantities, or modifier relationships. **Check or fix:** Rewrap actual text manually at each delivery layout. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** typographic, educational.

- **AVQ-020-006** Is the typographic hierarchy showing the main claim before its supporting detail? **Failure hypothesis:** Equal size and weight leave viewers without a reading order. **Check or fix:** Reduce secondary emphasis and test the first item readers notice. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-007** Does the animation finish forming each word before the viewer needs to act on it? **Failure hypothesis:** Letter-by-letter motion delays access to essential instruction. **Check or fix:** Reveal urgent instructions as complete phrases and retime the action. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** typographic, educational.

- **AVQ-020-008** Are number formatting and units unambiguous for the intended audience? **Failure hypothesis:** Locale conventions or missing units change the apparent magnitude. **Check or fix:** Specify units and consistent separators in the exact on-screen values. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-020-009** Does justified or stretched text create word spacing that changes readability at short exposure times? **Failure hypothesis:** Artificial spacing breaks word recognition. **Check or fix:** Use natural spacing and compare brief-exposure reading. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-010** Is text positioned inside the actual safe region after reframing and player overlays? **Failure hypothesis:** Reframing or controls cover critical words. **Check or fix:** Preview platform framing and move essentials into verified safe space. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-011** Do labels stay attached to the objects they describe during camera motion? **Failure hypothesis:** Screen-space labels drift away from scene-space targets. **Check or fix:** Track attachment points through the full move or use stable leader lines. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** vector, educational, 3d.

- **AVQ-020-012** Are leader lines terminating on distinct targets rather than crossing or pointing into empty space? **Failure hypothesis:** Annotation geometry creates incorrect object associations. **Check or fix:** Inspect the crowded state and reroute ambiguous connectors. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** educational, 3d, vector.

- **AVQ-020-013** Does a kinetic word change its meaning through cropping or motion before the full phrase is available? **Failure hypothesis:** Moving fragments create an unintended statement. **Check or fix:** Inspect every intermediate readable fragment and revise the animation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-014** Are minus signs, decimal points, superscripts, and exponents visible after scaling? **Failure hypothesis:** Small mathematical marks disappear while surrounding digits remain readable. **Check or fix:** Verify each critical mark in the delivered encode at target size. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, typographic.
