- **AVQ-020-015** Is a decorative display face being used for paragraphs that require sustained reading? **Failure hypothesis:** Ornament impairs word recognition over longer passages. **Check or fix:** Reserve the display face for short emphasis and simplify body type. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-016** Do font fallbacks preserve both meaning and layout when the project is reopened elsewhere? **Failure hypothesis:** Missing fonts change glyphs, wrapping, or timing. **Check or fix:** Reopen in a clean environment and compare rendered text extents. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-017** Does animated emphasis land on the intended word without reversing the sentence's meaning? **Failure hypothesis:** Highlight timing stresses a qualifier or negation incorrectly. **Check or fix:** Review the sentence with its actual emphasis sequence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** typographic, educational.

- **AVQ-020-018** Are quotation marks, attribution, and paraphrase styling clear enough to distinguish someone else's words from the narrator's? **Failure hypothesis:** Typography blurs quotation and authorial assertion. **Check or fix:** Apply a consistent quotation treatment and verify exact attribution. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, edit. **Style filters:** educational, live-action.

- **AVQ-020-019** Does the text remain crisp when transformed, or is a low-resolution raster layer being enlarged? **Failure hypothesis:** Typography is scaled beyond its raster source resolution. **Check or fix:** Render at final size or preserve vector text through compositing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** vector, typographic.

- **AVQ-020-020** Are right-to-left runs, mixed-direction numbers, or combined scripts displayed in the correct order? **Failure hypothesis:** Shaping or bidirectional handling rearranges meaning. **Check or fix:** Have a fluent reader inspect the exact rendered strings. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-021** Do accented characters and non-Latin glyphs match the visual weight and baseline of surrounding text? **Failure hypothesis:** Partial font coverage produces conspicuous fallback glyphs. **Check or fix:** Choose full-coverage fonts and inspect representative strings. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-022** Are automatically emphasized keywords actually important in context, including when they appear in negations or quotations? **Failure hypothesis:** Keyword matching highlights words whose local meaning should not receive emphasis. **Check or fix:** Review every emphasis match in its complete sentence and correct semantic false positives. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational, typographic.

- **AVQ-020-023** Is a title's visual treatment making a tentative claim look definitive? **Failure hypothesis:** Bold headline hierarchy suppresses uncertainty qualifiers. **Check or fix:** Place the scope and qualifier within the same readable hierarchy. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-020-024** Does text cast or receive effects that reduce the counters and internal spaces needed for reading? **Failure hypothesis:** Glow, shadow, or extrusion closes letter shapes. **Check or fix:** Compare effect-free and treated text at delivery size. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** typographic, 3d.

- **AVQ-020-025** Are tabular numbers aligned so viewers can compare place value rather than infer from uneven glyph widths? **Failure hypothesis:** Proportional spacing disguises numerical differences. **Check or fix:** Use tabular figures or a clearly aligned layout. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational, typographic.

- **AVQ-020-026** Does auto-fitting silently shrink the most important or longest message below the approved minimum? **Failure hypothesis:** Fit-to-box logic prioritizes containment over readability. **Check or fix:** Log fitted sizes and redesign outlier text instead of shrinking indefinitely. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-027** Are labels introduced with terminology that matches the narration and later references? **Failure hypothesis:** Synonyms look like separate concepts in a short film. **Check or fix:** Reconcile the spoken and displayed term lists. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** script, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-020-028** Does perspective text become unreadable at oblique angles precisely when its information is needed? **Failure hypothesis:** Scene-integrated typography sacrifices legibility to perspective. **Check or fix:** Adjust the camera, text plane, or repeat essentials in a readable view. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** 3d, live-action.

- **AVQ-020-029** Is the visual reading order preserved when text is rearranged for a vertical version? **Failure hypothesis:** Responsive placement changes the intended argument sequence. **Check or fix:** Read each format independently and revise its hierarchy. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, delivery. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-020-030** Does a legal or explanatory footnote contradict the prominent message while being too small to notice? **Failure hypothesis:** Fine print is used to repair an overbroad headline. **Check or fix:** Correct the main statement and make material limitations directly readable. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-020-031** Are important words ever partially hidden by decorative foreground elements during their reading interval? **Failure hypothesis:** Layer order allows ornament to occlude essential text. **Check or fix:** Inspect text visibility masks across the entire interval. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-020-032** Can the title be shortened without losing the specific promise, leaving more screen space for the actual evidence? **Failure hypothesis:** Verbose wording consumes the image needed to support the claim. **Check or fix:** Test a concise title alongside a larger evidence region. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** any.

### D021 captions and accessibility

- **AVQ-021-001** Do captions preserve every meaning-changing negation, name, and number in the actual spoken track? **Failure hypothesis:** Automated transcription drops small words that reverse meaning. **Check or fix:** Listen to the final track against captions and correct consequential mismatches. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-002** Are caption boundaries aligned to complete thought groups rather than arbitrary word counts? **Failure hypothesis:** Fragmented captions force viewers to reconstruct syntax under time pressure. **Check or fix:** Re-segment by spoken phrasing and test an uninterrupted read-through. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-003** Can viewers identify an off-screen speaker when the voice alone is not available? **Failure hypothesis:** Untagged dialogue becomes unattributable with sound off. **Check or fix:** Add concise speaker identifiers at ambiguous entrances. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction.

- **AVQ-021-004** Are story-relevant sounds described when no visible action conveys them? **Failure hypothesis:** A silent viewer misses an unseen alarm, knock, or approach. **Check or fix:** Listen for consequential sounds and add concise non-speech captions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-005** Do captions reveal the answer or punchline before the corresponding performance delivers it? **Failure hypothesis:** Early cue display spoils suspense or puzzle fairness. **Check or fix:** Shift cue boundaries so essential reveal words arrive at their intended moment. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle, fiction.

- **AVQ-021-006** Do captions cover faces, hands, demonstrations, or puzzle targets at any moment? **Failure hypothesis:** A fixed caption region obstructs essential visual evidence. **Check or fix:** Review full-length overlay playback and relocate captions at conflicts. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-007** Are captions still present and correctly timed after the final trim, speed change, or inserted slate? **Failure hypothesis:** Subtitle timestamps belong to an earlier edit. **Check or fix:** Validate the caption track against the exact delivered file. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-008** Does the caption display duration allow the intended audience to read the longest cue comfortably? **Failure hypothesis:** Nominal caption presence conceals unreadably fast exposure. **Check or fix:** Test long cues with target readers and split or extend them. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-009** Are overlapping voices represented without implying an order that was not spoken? **Failure hypothesis:** Captions serialize simultaneous speech into a false exchange. **Check or fix:** Listen to overlap passages and use clear multi-speaker formatting. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction.

- **AVQ-021-010** Do automatic captions invent speech during silence, music, or unintelligible audio? **Failure hypothesis:** Recognition output hallucinates dialogue where none is recoverable. **Check or fix:** Listen to low-confidence sections and remove unsupported text. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-011** Can a viewer understand the essential argument with sound muted, including any instruction delivered only by voice? **Failure hypothesis:** Captions omit or summarize away necessary spoken reasoning. **Check or fix:** Watch the actual muted delivery and attempt the stated viewer task. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-021-012** Is the audio description conveying crucial visual changes during gaps without competing with dialogue? **Failure hypothesis:** Description either omits essential action or masks existing speech. **Check or fix:** Listen to a description mix with blind or low-vision reviewers when available. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-013** Do instructions rely on color alone, such as 'follow the red line,' without a second identifier? **Failure hypothesis:** Viewers cannot identify the referent without the intended color distinction. **Check or fix:** Add position, pattern, or explicit labels to color-based references. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-021-014** Does rapid flashing or a strong alternating pattern require a qualified photosensitivity review before release? **Failure hypothesis:** Visually intense effects may pose a hazard missed by aesthetic review. **Check or fix:** Run an appropriate flash analysis and review flagged intervals before approval. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-015** Can prolonged camera sway, rapid zooms, or full-field rotation be reduced without losing the explanation? **Failure hypothesis:** Unnecessary global motion creates avoidable discomfort. **Check or fix:** Review motion-heavy intervals with sensitive viewers and test a calmer version. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-016** Are essential captions supplied as usable text tracks when the delivery context needs viewer control or search? **Failure hypothesis:** Burned-in text alone blocks selectable language or caption preferences. **Check or fix:** Deliver verified sidecar or embedded tracks supported by the destination. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-017** Do translated captions preserve technical meaning rather than mirror source-language word order? **Failure hypothesis:** Literal translation distorts a key relation or instruction. **Check or fix:** Have a fluent domain-capable reader review the actual rendered translation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-021-018** Is the caption track labeled with the correct language and accessibility purpose? **Failure hypothesis:** Incorrect metadata makes viewers choose an unsuitable track. **Check or fix:** Inspect track labels in the actual delivery player. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-019** Are meaningful changes in tone, such as sarcasm or whispering, recoverable when they affect the story? **Failure hypothesis:** Literal words conceal the intended social meaning. **Check or fix:** Add restrained delivery cues only where the plot depends on them. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-021-020** Can viewers pause on a dense diagram without its labels or controls disappearing? **Failure hypothesis:** Essential information depends on transient UI or animated states. **Check or fix:** Test pause and seek behavior at the densest explanatory frame. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-021-021** Are visual indications of success and failure distinguishable without an audio cue? **Failure hypothesis:** Feedback meaning is available only through a chime or buzzer. **Check or fix:** Add clearly different visual outcomes and test muted playback. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording, puzzle.

- **AVQ-021-022** Does narration describe a spatial relation precisely enough for a listener who cannot inspect the diagram? **Failure hypothesis:** Phrases such as 'over here' have no nonvisual referent. **Check or fix:** Replace deictic wording with meaningful spatial or structural descriptions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-021-023** Can viewers with less topic vocabulary follow the caption terminology without pausing for definitions? **Failure hypothesis:** Unexplained jargon creates an access barrier unrelated to the lesson. **Check or fix:** Define necessary terms at first use and test with intended novices. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-021-024** Do caption italics, positioning, or color conventions remain understandable when the player ignores their styling? **Failure hypothesis:** Semantic distinctions depend on formatting the player may discard. **Check or fix:** Test a plain-text fallback and add textual identifiers where necessary. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-025** Is a visible countdown accessible to someone relying on audio or captions without overwhelming the task? **Failure hypothesis:** Remaining response time is communicated in only one sensory channel. **Check or fix:** Provide a restrained redundant time cue and test task completion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-021-026** Are instructions given before an irreversible visual event rather than only while it is happening? **Failure hypothesis:** Slower readers receive the rule after the opportunity to use it. **Check or fix:** Move the instruction earlier and separate it from execution. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-021-027** Does a picture-in-picture sign-language window remain large enough and unobstructed for its intended viewers? **Failure hypothesis:** The access layer is reduced or cropped until signing details are unreadable. **Check or fix:** Review final-size playback with a qualified signer. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-028** Are lyrics that carry narrative information captioned distinctly from spoken dialogue? **Failure hypothesis:** Sung words disappear from access tracks or are mistaken for speech. **Check or fix:** Listen to relevant lyrics and include accurate, clearly identified cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-021-029** Does dense simultaneous motion, captioning, and sound make the central task harder for sensory-sensitive viewers? **Failure hypothesis:** Multiple salience peaks compete without a clear priority. **Check or fix:** Remove nonessential stimulation and compare task performance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-030** Are accessibility claims limited to checks actually performed on the delivered version? **Failure hypothesis:** A caption file is treated as proof of comprehensive accessibility. **Check or fix:** List completed checks, reviewer scope, and untested needs explicitly. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-021-031** Does an emergency or safety instruction retain its full meaning across captions, narration, and visual symbols? **Failure hypothesis:** One access channel drops a condition that makes the advice safe. **Check or fix:** Reconcile all channels against the verified instruction before release. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-021-032** Can a transcript substitute preserve references to important figures, sounds, and actions rather than just dialogue? **Failure hypothesis:** A dialogue-only transcript loses the evidence used by the film. **Check or fix:** Include concise descriptions at the relevant points and test independent reading. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

### D022 music composition

- **AVQ-022-001** Does the melody leave enough rhythmic space for the spoken explanation to remain easy to follow? **Failure hypothesis:** Dense melodic activity competes with speech segmentation. **Check or fix:** Listen to the full mix and simplify the melody during information-heavy passages. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-002** Is the harmonic change supporting the intended emotional turn rather than announcing a different mood? **Failure hypothesis:** Chord movement contradicts the character's visible change. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and compare an alternate harmonic treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-003** Does a repeated motif become meaningful through its association with a character or idea? **Failure hypothesis:** A recurring tune has no stable narrative association. **Check or fix:** Listen across all appearances and define or remove its recurring role. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-004** Is the cue's musical climax arriving at the story's decisive event? **Failure hypothesis:** The strongest musical peak precedes or follows the real payoff. **Check or fix:** Listen through the full arc and reshape the cue around the decisive beat. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-005** Does the opening establish an appropriate musical expectation for the kind of film that follows? **Failure hypothesis:** A grand or frantic intro promises an incompatible experience. **Check or fix:** Listen to opening and body consecutively with unbriefed viewers. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-006** Can the main rhythmic pulse still be understood when decorative percussion is removed? **Failure hypothesis:** Ornament conceals an unstable or unintelligible rhythmic foundation. **Check or fix:** Listen to the stripped rhythm and rebuild accents around a clear pulse. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-007** Does the groove feel intentionally relaxed or driving rather than accidentally dragging against the performance? **Failure hypothesis:** Musical microtiming and visual performance imply incompatible energy. **Check or fix:** Listen with the performance and adjust swing or note placement. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-008** Are phrase lengths compatible with the film's editorial units without forcing unnatural cuts? **Failure hypothesis:** The arrangement requires cutting important scenes merely to reach a cadence. **Check or fix:** Listen to phrase boundaries and recompose or extend the cue instead. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-009** Does a looping cue preserve melodic and harmonic continuity at the actual loop join? **Failure hypothesis:** The loop returns to an unresolved or incompatible musical state. **Check or fix:** Listen repeatedly across the join and rewrite the turnaround if needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-010** Does a long sustained harmony become fatiguing before the scene has completed its purpose? **Failure hypothesis:** An unchanging bed creates tension or monotony unrelated to the content. **Check or fix:** Listen for the full scene and introduce purposeful change or silence. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-011** Are bass movement and chord voicing reinforcing the intended tonal center? **Failure hypothesis:** Conflicting roots or voicings create unintended harmonic ambiguity. **Check or fix:** Listen to bass and harmony together and resolve accidental clashes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-012** Is a dissonant accent purposeful and resolved in a way the audience can experience? **Failure hypothesis:** A stray note sounds like an error rather than expressive tension. **Check or fix:** Listen in context and either develop or correct the dissonance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-013** Does the instrumentation fit the depicted scale and intimacy of the scene? **Failure hypothesis:** Oversized orchestration overwhelms a small gesture or quiet explanation. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and test a reduced ensemble. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-014** Is musical variation adding a new emotional or structural function rather than merely changing instruments? **Failure hypothesis:** Cosmetic arrangement swaps are mistaken for development. **Check or fix:** Listen across sections and name the changed musical purpose of each. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-015** Does the cue allow genuine silence where the story needs vulnerability, surprise, or attention? **Failure hypothesis:** Continuous scoring prevents meaningful contrast. **Check or fix:** Audition selected unscored beats against the full cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-016** Are tension-building devices released after the conflict resolves? **Failure hypothesis:** Rising textures continue into the resolution and imply unresolved danger. **Check or fix:** Listen across the resolution and remove or transform lingering tension layers. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-017** Does the ending cadence sound intentional rather than like an arbitrary fade in the middle of a phrase? **Failure hypothesis:** Runtime trimming interrupts the composition's closure. **Check or fix:** Listen without picture and write a coherent ending within the available time. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-018** Can a sparse cue sustain interest through articulation, dynamics, or timbre without adding unnecessary notes? **Failure hypothesis:** Minimalism is implemented as inert repetition. **Check or fix:** Listen for evolving detail and shape a restrained expressive arc. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-019** Do contrasting musical sections still belong to the same film's sound world? **Failure hypothesis:** Unrelated patches or styles make transitions feel like stock-track swaps. **Check or fix:** Listen to cue boundaries and establish a shared motif or sonic palette. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-020** Does a comic sting strengthen the visible joke without explaining it before the audience discovers it? **Failure hypothesis:** Musical punctuation telegraphs the joke too early. **Check or fix:** Listen with unbriefed viewers and move or simplify the sting. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-022-021** Are melodic leaps and sustained notes plausible for the instrument or voice being represented? **Failure hypothesis:** Generated writing exceeds the believable articulation or range. **Check or fix:** Listen with an instrument-aware reviewer and revise exposed implausible passages. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-022** Does the arrangement avoid having every instrument accent the same event at full intensity? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform emphasis removes internal depth and creates musical clutter. **Check or fix:** Listen by section and assign distinct foreground and support roles. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-023** Is rhythmic syncopation legible at the cue's actual tempo rather than only in the notation? **Failure hypothesis:** Fast off-beat detail becomes an indistinct flurry. **Check or fix:** Listen at delivery speed and simplify patterns that lose their intended groove. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-024** Does tempo development follow a deliberate arc rather than drift because generated segments were joined? **Failure hypothesis:** Segment assembly produces unintended tempo changes. **Check or fix:** Listen across joins and compare measured beat intervals with the intended map. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-025** Are important melodic notes masked compositionally by competing lines in the same register? **Failure hypothesis:** Counterlines obscure the theme before mixing can establish hierarchy. **Check or fix:** Listen to the arrangement and move or simplify competing registers. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-026** Does the music's cultural reference support the setting without relying on a shallow sonic stereotype? **Failure hypothesis:** Token instrumentation substitutes for researched musical context. **Check or fix:** Listen with informed reviewers and revise unsupported stylistic shorthand. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-027** Is an educational comparison using identical musical treatment where emotion should not bias the conclusion? **Failure hypothesis:** One option sounds heroic and the other defective despite neutral evidence. **Check or fix:** Listen blind to each treatment and neutralize unintended evaluative cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-022-028** Does a purportedly cheerful cue contain an ominous undertone that changes the audience's reading of the scene? **Failure hypothesis:** Register, harmony, or texture communicates unintended threat. **Check or fix:** Listen with unbriefed viewers and revise the conflicting musical layer. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-029** Are dynamic changes shaped as musical phrases rather than constant global volume pumping? **Failure hypothesis:** Uniform automation substitutes for expressive articulation. **Check or fix:** Listen to phrases and adjust note-level or section-level dynamics. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-030** Can the cue survive removal of a fashionable sound without losing its identity? **Failure hypothesis:** The composition relies entirely on a transient production cliché. **Check or fix:** Listen to a neutral-instrument version and strengthen motif or structure. **Severity:** low. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-031** Does a generated cue repeat an obvious glitch or malformed phrase that the full arrangement initially distracts from? **Failure hypothesis:** Local generation artifacts recur beneath otherwise plausible music. **Check or fix:** Listen closely to exposed and repeated passages and repair the affected phrase. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-022-032** What specific musical contribution is lost if this cue is replaced with silence during the key scene? **Failure hypothesis:** Music is present by habit without a defensible storytelling role. **Check or fix:** Listen to both versions and retain only a supported emotional or structural function. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

### D023 voice and dialogue

- **AVQ-023-001** Can an unbriefed listener transcribe the sentence containing the film's main claim correctly? **Failure hypothesis:** Familiarity with the script masks unclear speech. **Check or fix:** Play the final voice track to new listeners and compare their transcriptions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-002** Is vocal stress placed on the word that carries the intended contrast? **Failure hypothesis:** Incorrect emphasis changes which alternative the sentence rejects. **Check or fix:** Listen without reading the script and reperform ambiguous stress. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-003** Are proper names and specialist terms pronounced accurately for the subject and audience? **Failure hypothesis:** Synthetic or unfamiliar pronunciation undermines meaning and credibility. **Check or fix:** Listen with a knowledgeable speaker and correct the specific pronunciation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-004** Do numbers, ranges, and units remain distinguishable when spoken at the final pace? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar-sounding quantities merge into an ambiguous instruction. **Check or fix:** Have listeners write down the quantities and re-record errors. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-005** Does the narration sound as though it understands the sentence rather than reading punctuation mechanically? **Failure hypothesis:** Prosodic breaks interrupt the sentence's logical structure. **Check or fix:** Listen phrase by phrase and revise punctuation or performance. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-006** Are breath pauses placed where a human speaker could plausibly breathe without splitting essential phrases? **Failure hypothesis:** Generated or edited speech has unnatural respiration and phrasing. **Check or fix:** Listen through full sentences and restore plausible breath placement. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-007** Does the narrator's emotional intensity fit the evidence rather than oversell an ordinary result? **Failure hypothesis:** Excited delivery implies certainty or significance not supported by the film. **Check or fix:** Listen blind to the claim and moderate unsupported enthusiasm. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-008** Can the audience distinguish recurring speakers without needing to look at the screen continuously? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar voices make the conversation hard to follow. **Check or fix:** Listen audio-only and differentiate delivery or add clear attribution. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-009** Is the same character's voice consistent across separately generated or recorded lines? **Failure hypothesis:** Timbre, accent, or age changes imply a different speaker. **Check or fix:** Audition adjacent and distant lines together and replace inconsistent takes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-010** Does a change from narration to character speech sound intentional? **Failure hypothesis:** One delivery style blurs narrator and story-world dialogue. **Check or fix:** Listen to entrances and establish a distinct, consistent performance convention. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-011** Are plosive bursts obscuring the beginnings of important words? **Failure hypothesis:** Low-frequency microphone blasts mask consonants and distract. **Check or fix:** Listen on full-range playback and repair or rerecord affected words. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-012** Does sibilance become piercing on actual listening even when meters show no clipping? **Failure hypothesis:** High-frequency consonant energy causes discomfort. **Check or fix:** Audition on representative headphones and apply selective de-essing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-013** Are mouth clicks or lip noises exposed in quiet passages enough to distract from the message? **Failure hypothesis:** Close-miked artifacts dominate a sparse soundtrack. **Check or fix:** Listen to quiet intervals and remove artifacts without damaging speech. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-014** Does noise reduction distort consonant edges or leave watery modulation around the voice? **Failure hypothesis:** Aggressive cleanup trades background noise for speech artifacts. **Check or fix:** Listen to original and processed takes and reduce processing where needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-015** Do edits between takes change room tone, vocal distance, or vocal effort within one sentence? **Failure hypothesis:** Spliced speech sounds physically discontinuous. **Check or fix:** Listen across every splice and choose compatible takes or natural boundaries. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-016** Is a whispered or subdued line still understandable at the intended playback level? **Failure hypothesis:** Acting choice removes words essential to the plot. **Check or fix:** Listen at ordinary volume and improve delivery or mix without changing intent. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-017** Does dialogue overlap communicate a motivated interruption rather than accidental timing congestion? **Failure hypothesis:** Simultaneous lines obscure both intention and content. **Check or fix:** Listen with the scene and stagger or clarify the competing turns. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-018** Do the character's words and vocal emotion agree with the visible reaction at that moment? **Failure hypothesis:** Performance communicates an emotion the image does not support. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and reconcile the take with the reaction. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-019** Can listeners hear a meaning-changing negation clearly when it falls between louder words? **Failure hypothesis:** Reduced articulation makes 'not' or equivalent disappear. **Check or fix:** Audition the exact phrase with new listeners and strengthen the negation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-020** Are acronyms expanded, spoken, or spelled consistently with how the intended audience recognizes them? **Failure hypothesis:** The voice invents an unfamiliar reading of a technical abbreviation. **Check or fix:** Listen with domain users and standardize the acronym treatment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-021** Does switching languages preserve a credible pronunciation and speaking rhythm in each language? **Failure hypothesis:** Multilingual delivery distorts one language while sounding fluent in another. **Check or fix:** Obtain fluent listening review for every language segment. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-022** Is an accent being performed intelligibly without caricature or arbitrary inconsistency? **Failure hypothesis:** Exaggerated accent cues distract or stereotype the character. **Check or fix:** Listen with informed speakers and revise unsupported performance choices. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-023** Do pauses around a quote make its beginning and end audible without visual punctuation? **Failure hypothesis:** Listeners misattribute quoted words to the narrator. **Check or fix:** Listen audio-only and add natural attribution or clear vocal framing. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** educational, live-action.

- **AVQ-023-024** Does removing filler words accidentally make a hesitant or uncertain speaker sound more confident than they were? **Failure hypothesis:** Cleanup changes the meaning of an authentic statement. **Check or fix:** Listen to source and edited context and preserve consequential hesitation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action.

- **AVQ-023-025** Is speech sped up enough to sound strained or erase the narrator's natural cadence? **Failure hypothesis:** Duration fitting sacrifices intelligibility and believable delivery. **Check or fix:** Listen at final speed and shorten the script instead of overcompressing speech. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-026** Does the narrator address the intended audience with an appropriate level of familiarity and authority? **Failure hypothesis:** Delivery assumes expertise, age, or intimacy the audience does not share. **Check or fix:** Listen with representative viewers and adjust register rather than only vocabulary. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-027** Are sentence endings audible rather than trailing below the noise floor or music bed? **Failure hypothesis:** Important qualifiers are lost in fading vocal energy. **Check or fix:** Listen to endings in the full mix and repair delivery or automation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-023-028** Does the dialogue give each character a distinct objective instead of making every line explain the plot? **Failure hypothesis:** Exposition replaces interaction and weakens performance. **Check or fix:** Read aloud and listen for lines no character would need to say. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-029** Does the voice's apparent microphone perspective remain compatible with a supposedly continuous scene? **Failure hypothesis:** Dry close speech abruptly becomes distant without a scene change. **Check or fix:** Listen consecutively and align recording or processing perspective. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction.

- **AVQ-023-030** Are generated breaths, laughs, or emotional interjections convincing in their immediate context? **Failure hypothesis:** Synthetic nonverbal sounds break character believability. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and replace or remove implausible interjections. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-023-031** Does a long narration segment develop vocal energy naturally instead of resetting to the same opening cadence each sentence? **Failure hypothesis:** Sentence-by-sentence generation produces a repetitive presentation pattern. **Check or fix:** Listen to the complete paragraph and reperform it as a coherent thought. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-023-032** Is there any spoken instruction or claim in the delivered voice that is absent from the approved script? **Failure hypothesis:** Ad-libs or generation artifacts introduce unreviewed content. **Check or fix:** Listen end to end while reconciling the delivered transcript to the script. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D024 sfx and mix

- **AVQ-024-001** Does the delivered mix contain audible distortion at impacts or other peaks despite an unclipped project meter? **Failure hypothesis:** Encoding or intersample peaks distort the exported waveform. **Check or fix:** Measure the final file and actually listen to its loudest events. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-002** Is dialogue intelligible under the busiest music and effects passage at ordinary listening volume? **Failure hypothesis:** Masking peaks hide speech despite acceptable overall loudness. **Check or fix:** Listen to the complete mix and rebalance the busiest interval. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-003** Does dialogue ducking recover naturally after speech rather than making the music audibly surge between words? **Failure hypothesis:** Overfast gain recovery causes distracting pumping. **Check or fix:** Listen across short speech gaps and revise ducking timing. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-004** Does the mix retain essential sounds when summed to mono? **Failure hypothesis:** Phase cancellation removes cues on single-speaker playback. **Check or fix:** Audition a mono downmix and repair phase-dependent elements. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-005** Are low-frequency effects crowding out clarity or wasting headroom without an audible storytelling benefit? **Failure hypothesis:** Sub-bass accumulation drives limiting while contributing little useful sound. **Check or fix:** Listen full-range and on phone speakers, then trim unsupported low end. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-006** Does the soundtrack remain balanced on the actual phone or laptop speakers likely to be used? **Failure hypothesis:** A studio-only mix depends on bass and detail small speakers cannot reproduce. **Check or fix:** Listen on representative devices and rebalance essential cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-007** Are important off-screen sounds too far to one stereo side to be heard reliably with one earbud? **Failure hypothesis:** Hard panning makes a narrative cue fragile in common listening conditions. **Check or fix:** Audition each channel alone and reposition or reinforce essential cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-008** Does ambience continue naturally through cuts within the same place and time? **Failure hypothesis:** Room or environmental tone resets expose edits and break continuity. **Check or fix:** Listen without picture across cuts and smooth justified ambience transitions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction.

- **AVQ-024-009** Do noise gates audibly chop breaths, room tone, or sustained sound tails? **Failure hypothesis:** Threshold processing creates abrupt silence and damaged decay. **Check or fix:** Listen to soft endings and ease gating or use manual cleanup. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-010** Is reverberation appropriate to the apparent room size and source distance? **Failure hypothesis:** Acoustic space contradicts the visual environment. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and adjust early reflections and decay contextually. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-011** Are footsteps, touches, and object movements giving the represented material and weight a plausible sound? **Failure hypothesis:** Generic effects make soft, heavy, or hollow objects sound alike. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and replace mismatched material cues. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-012** Does a major impact have a distinct onset and decay rather than becoming an undifferentiated wall of effects? **Failure hypothesis:** Layer stacking removes the event's audible shape. **Check or fix:** Audition effect layers separately and restore a clear attack/body/tail structure. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-013** Are repeated effects varying in a way that avoids machine-like repetition while preserving the event's identity? **Failure hypothesis:** Identical samples reveal the production pattern. **Check or fix:** Listen to the entire repeated sequence and vary suitable takes or articulation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-014** Does a sound continue after its source has visibly stopped for a physically or narratively justified reason? **Failure hypothesis:** Untrimmed effects imply motion or activity no longer present. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and align source cessation and credible decay. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-015** Are quiet atmospheric details audible enough to support the scene without making viewers raise volume dangerously before loud events? **Failure hypothesis:** Excessive dynamic range creates an impractical listening experience. **Check or fix:** Listen at one fixed normal level and rebalance essential quiet and loud passages. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-016** Does compression preserve the punch of intentional transients rather than flatten every action? **Failure hypothesis:** Heavy limiting removes contrast and makes impacts feel weak. **Check or fix:** Listen to level-matched processed and unprocessed versions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-017** Is there a harsh resonant frequency that becomes tiring over the full runtime? **Failure hypothesis:** Narrowband buildup is tolerable briefly but fatiguing across the film. **Check or fix:** Listen continuously and reduce the identified resonance selectively. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-018** Do looped ambience beds have audible seams or repeated identifiable events? **Failure hypothesis:** A background loop reveals itself through a recurring chirp, click, or level jump. **Check or fix:** Listen across multiple loops and edit or vary the bed. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-019** Are source distance changes accompanied by believable changes in level, detail, and room contribution? **Failure hypothesis:** A moving source sounds fixed next to the listener. **Check or fix:** Listen with the movement and automate acoustic perspective deliberately. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-020** Does the mix make a decorative sound more prominent than the story's causal sound? **Failure hypothesis:** Salient ornament distracts from the event viewers must understand. **Check or fix:** Listen without picture to identify the perceived foreground, then rebalance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-021** Are left and right channels mapped correctly for directional cues? **Failure hypothesis:** A routing swap contradicts on-screen movement. **Check or fix:** Listen to a known directional sequence in the delivered file. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-022** Does a surround or multichannel mix downmix without losing center dialogue or overemphasizing effects? **Failure hypothesis:** Unverified downmix rules alter the intended hierarchy. **Check or fix:** Audition destination downmixes and correct channel assignment or levels. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-023** Are accidental clicks introduced at edits, fades, or sample boundaries? **Failure hypothesis:** Discontinuous waveforms create transient artifacts. **Check or fix:** Listen to edit boundaries closely and repair fades or zero crossings. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-024** Does hiss, hum, or electrical buzz emerge during sparse sections where it was previously masked? **Failure hypothesis:** Background contamination becomes audible when the arrangement thins. **Check or fix:** Listen to quiet full-mix passages and remove the specific contamination. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-025** Is an effect being used to imply a real mechanism the image does not demonstrate? **Failure hypothesis:** Sound supplies false evidence of contact, locking, charging, or completion. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and verify the physical event behind each causal cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-024-026** Does an abrupt loud sound startle the audience beyond what the scene or audience context warrants? **Failure hypothesis:** Unmotivated level contrast creates avoidable distress or discomfort. **Check or fix:** Listen at ordinary volume and soften or prepare the cue where appropriate. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-027** Is the soundtrack's loudness being judged on the final encode rather than solely on a premaster? **Failure hypothesis:** Later encoding, normalization, or replacement changes the delivered balance. **Check or fix:** Measure and listen to the exact delivered version. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-028** Do effect layers occupy complementary spectral regions instead of multiplying the same muddy band? **Failure hypothesis:** Layering increases density without adding perceptual detail. **Check or fix:** Listen to isolated and combined layers and remove redundant material. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** production, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-029** Are intentional periods of silence clean and shaped, rather than revealing sudden residual noise or truncated tails? **Failure hypothesis:** Mute automation creates conspicuous technical gaps. **Check or fix:** Listen through silence entries and exits and shape the transitions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-030** Can listeners tell when a sound belongs to the scene versus a nonliteral editorial accent? **Failure hypothesis:** Sound design confuses real-world events with commentary. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and establish a consistent sonic distinction. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-031** Does the final audio contain unintended preview tags, spoken watermarks, or test tones? **Failure hypothesis:** Temporary or uncleared production audio survives into delivery. **Check or fix:** Listen end to end and reconcile every audible element to the final asset list. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-024-032** Does normalizing a short clip hide a problematic internal jump between adjacent scenes? **Failure hypothesis:** Whole-file loudness compliance masks local imbalance. **Check or fix:** Listen across every scene boundary and adjust local balance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D025 audio visual timing

- **AVQ-025-001** Do visible lip closures coincide with the corresponding consonants in the delivered dialogue? **Failure hypothesis:** Mouth animation uses a nearby but perceptually wrong phoneme timing. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and inspect exposed consonants frame by frame. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction, 3d, cutout.

- **AVQ-025-002** Does a footstep sound land on weight-bearing contact rather than on the foot's lowest airborne position? **Failure hypothesis:** Naive motion markers misidentify the actual contact event. **Check or fix:** Listen with the walk and align cues to verified contact frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout, stop-motion, fiction, live-action.

- **AVQ-025-003** Are musical accents aligned to the perceptually important phase of a gesture rather than merely its keyframe timestamp? **Failure hypothesis:** Technical alignment misses the gesture's experienced peak. **Check or fix:** Listen at normal speed and compare anticipation, peak, and settle placements. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-025-004** Does synchronization drift progressively from the beginning to the end of the video? **Failure hypothesis:** Audio and picture were interpreted under different clock or rate assumptions. **Check or fix:** Compare early and late landmarks and listen through the delivered file. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-005** Is a constant global audio offset shifting every sound ahead of or behind the picture? **Failure hypothesis:** Container or timeline offset moves the entire soundtrack. **Check or fix:** Measure several independent landmarks and verify the correction by listening. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-006** When narration says 'this part,' is the intended object already visible and still highlighted? **Failure hypothesis:** Spoken reference and visual focus occupy different intervals. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and align each deictic phrase to its referent. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-025-007** Does a spoken explanation introduce a diagram feature before the feature changes beyond recognition? **Failure hypothesis:** The image advances while the voice still describes its prior state. **Check or fix:** Listen with the animation and hold the referenced state through the phrase. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-025-008** Are deliberate anticipatory sounds clearly nonliteral rather than suggesting an impact before contact? **Failure hypothesis:** An editorial whoosh is mistaken for a physically early collision. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and distinguish anticipation from the contact cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-009** Does an echo or repeated effect make one visible event seem like two separate actions? **Failure hypothesis:** Audible repetitions imply extra events absent from the picture. **Check or fix:** Listen without prior context and simplify ambiguous repeats. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-010** Does the audiovisual loop return to the same phase in both music and movement? **Failure hypothesis:** Unequal loop lengths cause a new phase mismatch on each repeat. **Check or fix:** Play multiple loops and compare shared start landmarks. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-011** Does speed-ramping footage preserve the intended relationship between source motion and sound duration? **Failure hypothesis:** Picture retiming leaves effects or dialogue at incompatible speeds. **Check or fix:** Listen across ramps and retime or replace sound intentionally. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-012** Are sharp audio transients still aligned after video frame-rate conversion quantizes the event time? **Failure hypothesis:** Conforming rounds visual events but leaves sample-accurate audio unchanged. **Check or fix:** Inspect converted landmarks and listen to the final cadence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-013** Does encoder priming or inserted silence delay the first audible event relative to the first visual action? **Failure hypothesis:** Codec padding alters the beginning of the delivered clip. **Check or fix:** Decode and listen from the start in target players. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-014** Is the last spoken word or sound decay audible before the encoded picture ends? **Failure hypothesis:** Mismatched stream durations truncate the soundtrack's conclusion. **Check or fix:** Listen through the actual endpoint and inspect both stream durations. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-015** Do separately rendered scene segments share one timeline rather than restarting local audio phase at each join? **Failure hypothesis:** Segment assembly resets a continuous cue or ambience cycle. **Check or fix:** Listen across all joins and compare segment offsets to the master timeline. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-016** Does apparent source motion across the screen agree with audible spatial movement? **Failure hypothesis:** Pan automation follows the wrong trajectory or direction. **Check or fix:** Listen with headphones and picture, then align spatial cues to the intended path. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-017** Is any intended sound-travel delay plausible and distinguishable from accidental desynchronization? **Failure hypothesis:** Delayed sound appears broken because distance or scale is not established. **Check or fix:** Listen with the shot and verify the delay rationale and visual distance cue. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational, live-action, 3d.

- **AVQ-025-018** Does a countdown's audible pulse indicate the same remaining time as its visible numerals? **Failure hypothesis:** Timer graphics and audio clicks are driven by separate clocks. **Check or fix:** Listen while stepping through numeral changes and reconcile the time authority. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-025-019** Are sound cues for simultaneous comparison panels using the same event definition on both sides? **Failure hypothesis:** One panel sounds at anticipation while the other sounds at completion. **Check or fix:** Listen to isolated panels and normalize the cue criterion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-025-020** Does an audio-reactive graphic actually derive from the delivered audio rather than a different draft? **Failure hypothesis:** A plausible waveform or meter displays unrelated signal data. **Check or fix:** Compare audio features to graphic timing and listen at mismatched peaks. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** screen-recording, educational, typographic.

- **AVQ-025-021** Does automatic beat detection identify the pulse listeners actually hear in this cue? **Failure hypothesis:** An estimator locks onto subdivisions or half-time accents. **Check or fix:** Listen and tap the pulse, then compare it with detected beats. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-022** Are repeated visual accents distributed musically when the beat period is not an integer number of video frames? **Failure hypothesis:** Naive rounding accumulates jitter or duplicates accent frames. **Check or fix:** Inspect frame scheduling and listen to the long repeated sequence. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** vector, typographic.

- **AVQ-025-023** Does a frozen image accompanied by continuing action sounds imply an event the viewer can no longer verify? **Failure hypothesis:** Audio continuation creates false off-screen progress during a freeze. **Check or fix:** Listen with picture and either justify the off-screen action or change the cue. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-025-024** Does reversing a shot leave sound envelopes that suggest forward action or an impossible sequence? **Failure hypothesis:** Reversed visuals retain incompatible attack and decay cues. **Check or fix:** Listen to the reversed sequence and redesign sound for the intended event. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-025** Are emotional vocal pauses coordinated with visible reactions instead of overwritten by unrelated movement? **Failure hypothesis:** The body advances while the voice is still processing the moment. **Check or fix:** Listen with performance and retime reaction beats around the spoken thought. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-025-026** Does a visual beat tied to spoken wording still work in a translated or alternate-voice version? **Failure hypothesis:** Different phrase lengths break an alignment inherited from the original. **Check or fix:** Listen to every language version with its actual picture timing. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-027** Is the audio track paired with the exact picture revision it was mixed against? **Failure hypothesis:** A correctly timed soundtrack is attached to a different cut. **Check or fix:** Verify version hashes and listen at each changed edit boundary. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-028** Does seeking or resuming playback introduce an audiovisual offset that continuous playback does not reveal? **Failure hypothesis:** Player buffering or timestamps cause transient desynchronization after navigation. **Check or fix:** Test real seek/resume behavior and listen at recognizable sync events. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-029** Are scene ambience changes synchronized with the intended acoustic boundary rather than arbitrary cut points? **Failure hypothesis:** Sound changes rooms before the character crosses the doorway. **Check or fix:** Listen with spatial action and place the acoustic transition at the justified moment. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** edit. **Style filters:** live-action, fiction, 3d.

- **AVQ-025-030** Does a note or effect trigger twice when a visual event sits on a segment or loop boundary? **Failure hypothesis:** Inclusive boundary logic schedules the same event in both adjacent blocks. **Check or fix:** Audit event IDs and listen across the affected boundary. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-031** Can the apparent synchronization be verified on the actual destination rather than inferred from an editor preview? **Failure hypothesis:** Preview playback compensation hides delivery-specific timing problems. **Check or fix:** Listen and watch the uploaded or delivered rendition on its real player. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-025-032** If a cue is deliberately off-beat, does that mismatch read as expressive timing rather than an error? **Failure hypothesis:** Intentional displacement is too weakly motivated to be perceived as design. **Check or fix:** Listen with unbriefed viewers and compare an aligned version. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

### D026 rights and asset provenance

- **AVQ-026-001** Can every imported asset be traced to the exact source file and license that applied when it was obtained? **Failure hypothesis:** A generic website reference is substituted for asset-specific permission. **Check or fix:** Build a file-hash ledger linked to retained license evidence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-002** Does the license permit the intended rendered use, including commercial or promotional distribution if planned? **Failure hypothesis:** A broadly labeled free asset has narrower use restrictions. **Check or fix:** Review the exact license against the declared distribution purpose. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-003** May the original asset be included in editable project delivery, rather than only in a finished render? **Failure hypothesis:** Native-source packaging redistributes material beyond render-only permission. **Check or fix:** Separate source-redistribution rights from rendered-output rights. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-004** Are music composition rights and sound-recording rights both resolved for the exact cue? **Failure hypothesis:** Permission for one rights layer is mistaken for permission for both. **Check or fix:** Identify the composition and recording owners and retain applicable grants. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-005** Do sampled sounds within a music track have provenance independent of the finished track's author claim? **Failure hypothesis:** A composer supplies music containing uncleared third-party samples. **Check or fix:** Request the sample inventory and evidence covering the intended use. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-006** Does a voice performance have permission for this use, including synthetic replication if that occurred? **Failure hypothesis:** Recording permission is stretched into unauthorized voice cloning or reuse. **Check or fix:** Check the performer grant against the actual generation and distribution method. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-007** Are recognizable people's likenesses used within the scope they authorized? **Failure hypothesis:** Footage or images are repurposed beyond the original consent. **Check or fix:** Match the planned context and audience to the relevant release. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** live-action, 3d.

- **AVQ-026-008** If a minor appears, is the permission record appropriate to that person's age and planned distribution? **Failure hypothesis:** Ordinary asset handling overlooks heightened consent requirements. **Check or fix:** Hold uncertain use for qualified review and verify the relevant authorization. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** live-action.

- **AVQ-026-009** Do visible trademarks or branded interfaces falsely suggest sponsorship, affiliation, or endorsement? **Failure hypothesis:** Context converts incidental branding into an implied commercial relationship. **Check or fix:** Review framing and wording and remove unsupported endorsement cues. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** live-action, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-026-010** Is a stock asset restricted to editorial use while the film serves a promotional purpose? **Failure hypothesis:** A convenient stock download is used in an incompatible context. **Check or fix:** Check the exact asset's license designation and replace if necessary. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-011** Are required credits present, accurate, and readable in the actual delivered package? **Failure hypothesis:** Attribution obligations are lost between working notes and export. **Check or fix:** Compare each required credit with the final video and accompanying files. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-012** Does cropping or editing an asset remove a notice or violate a restriction on modification? **Failure hypothesis:** Production changes strip required attribution or exceed permitted alterations. **Check or fix:** Compare the modified asset to its retained terms. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-013** Are font licenses sufficient for the render, embedded file, and editable source being delivered? **Failure hypothesis:** Desktop font use is mistaken for unrestricted font redistribution. **Check or fix:** Check each font's permitted output and embedding modes separately. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** typographic.

- **AVQ-026-014** Can a generated asset's model and service terms support the specific output use being claimed? **Failure hypothesis:** 'AI-generated' is treated as automatic unrestricted ownership. **Check or fix:** Record the actual model/service version and applicable output terms. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-015** Is any claim that training data or model outputs are fully rights-cleared supported by evidence? **Failure hypothesis:** An unsupported assurance is repeated as a legal guarantee. **Check or fix:** Limit claims to documented rights and disclose unresolved provenance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-016** Does a model or tool require a particular paid tier or organizational license for this use? **Failure hypothesis:** A technically available route exceeds the license actually held. **Check or fix:** Verify the installed tool's entitlement against the planned production. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-017** Are third-party plugins, scripts, or model weights being bundled only where redistribution is permitted? **Failure hypothesis:** A reproducibility package inadvertently redistributes restricted dependencies. **Check or fix:** Audit package contents and replace restricted binaries with acquisition instructions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.
