- **AVQ-026-018** Have license expiration, project limits, or download-credit conditions been checked for this particular deliverable? **Failure hypothesis:** Permission assumed from an old subscription does not cover the present use. **Check or fix:** Retain the applicable grant and verify its project and time scope. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-019** Are territory, platform, or audience restrictions compatible with every planned destination? **Failure hypothesis:** A cleared local use is silently expanded into a broader release. **Check or fix:** Compare each destination against the recorded permission scope. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-020** Does a derivative or adapted asset carry conditions that must survive into the combined work? **Failure hypothesis:** Adaptation obligations are lost during compositing or conversion. **Check or fix:** Identify derivative-use conditions and review the assembled delivery. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-021** Are the licenses of combined reusable assets compatible with how the project will be shared? **Failure hypothesis:** Separate permissive-looking grants impose conflicting redistribution conditions. **Check or fix:** Review the intended combined package before committing to its license. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-022** Can a reference-inspired design be distinguished from copying protected character, shot, or sequence expression? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface changes disguise close reproduction of a specific work. **Check or fix:** Compare expressive choices and redesign similarities that lack a cleared basis. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-023** Does a photographed location contain artwork or other protected material that needs separate consideration? **Failure hypothesis:** Location permission is mistaken for rights to every depicted work. **Check or fix:** Inventory prominent embedded works and resolve uncertain uses. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** live-action.

- **AVQ-026-024** Is permission to film on a property being confused with permission to promote or commercially associate with it? **Failure hypothesis:** A location release has a narrower purpose than the final presentation. **Check or fix:** Compare the final context with the actual property agreement. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** live-action.

- **AVQ-026-025** Have preview assets, watermarked downloads, and evaluation-only files been excluded from the final package? **Failure hypothesis:** Temporary production material is mistaken for licensed final content. **Check or fix:** Audit every final asset hash against the cleared inventory. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-026** Are original creators and collaborators credited according to the actual agreement rather than inferred from filenames? **Failure hypothesis:** Informal naming obscures authorship or contractual credit. **Check or fix:** Confirm contribution records and agreed credit wording. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-027** Does an asset's claimed public-domain status apply in the relevant jurisdiction and to this exact version? **Failure hypothesis:** A modern restoration, translation, or recording is conflated with an older work. **Check or fix:** Verify the specific version and obtain qualified review where uncertain. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-028** Are screenshots of private systems free of information the user did not authorize sharing? **Failure hypothesis:** Demonstration footage exposes personal or confidential content incidentally. **Check or fix:** Inspect full-resolution frames and mask or replace unauthorized information. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** screen-recording.

- **AVQ-026-029** Is a synthetic or staged performance labeled honestly where its origin affects the viewer's interpretation? **Failure hypothesis:** Generated footage is presented as authentic capture or testimony. **Check or fix:** Document production origin and add an appropriate clear disclosure. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, delivery. **Style filters:** 3d, stop-motion, live-action.

- **AVQ-026-030** If the same cleared asset is reused in another episode, does the permission cover that additional project? **Failure hypothesis:** Single-project rights are silently treated as a reusable-library license. **Check or fix:** Check project-count and reuse terms before reuse. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-031** Can reviewers distinguish creator-owned material from licensed material and unresolved material in the handoff? **Failure hypothesis:** Mixed provenance is flattened into a blanket ownership statement. **Check or fix:** Assign explicit provenance status to every deliverable component. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-026-032** Is the final rights signoff tied to the delivered asset versions rather than earlier approved substitutes? **Failure hypothesis:** Late asset swaps bypass the rights review. **Check or fix:** Reconcile final hashes and changes against the approved rights ledger. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

### D027 factual and algorithmic integrity

- **AVQ-027-001** Can each factual sentence be matched to a source that supports its exact scope rather than merely the general topic? **Failure hypothesis:** Broad source relevance is mistaken for support of a specific claim. **Check or fix:** Build a claim-to-source ledger with exact supporting passages. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-002** Are time-sensitive facts still valid at the date the video will be delivered? **Failure hypothesis:** A formerly accurate script becomes misleading through age. **Check or fix:** Recheck unstable claims against current authoritative sources before release. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** script, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-003** Does a historical claim match the exact locality, period, and object depicted? **Failure hypothesis:** A generally plausible prop is used as evidence for an overly specific claim. **Check or fix:** Verify the depicted variant and narrow unsupported specificity. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational, live-action.

- **AVQ-027-004** Are measurements and labels using consistent units and conversion assumptions throughout the film? **Failure hypothesis:** Numerically plausible values mix incompatible units. **Check or fix:** Independently recompute conversions and audit visible and spoken units. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** script, production, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-005** Does a percentage state its denominator and comparison baseline clearly enough to reproduce it? **Failure hypothesis:** A favorable percentage hides a changed denominator or base period. **Check or fix:** Show the relevant counts and recompute the displayed percentage. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-006** Are averages concealing variation that materially changes the lesson? **Failure hypothesis:** A central value is presented as typical despite a wide or skewed distribution. **Check or fix:** Inspect the distribution and add a relevant range or counterexample. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-007** Does the edit distinguish correlation from a demonstrated causal mechanism? **Failure hypothesis:** Associated events are narrated as cause and effect. **Check or fix:** Audit causal wording against the study or demonstration design. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-008** Is a counterfactual comparison supported by a valid method rather than an invented alternate outcome? **Failure hypothesis:** The film fabricates what would have happened without the intervention. **Check or fix:** State the method and assumptions or replace it with a verified worked case. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-009** Are uncertainty and limitations visible at the point where the audience forms the conclusion? **Failure hypothesis:** Caveats arrive too late to constrain an authoritative impression. **Check or fix:** Place material uncertainty beside the claim and test interpretation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-010** Are a diagram's idealizations clearly distinguished from measured or physically exact behavior? **Failure hypothesis:** Clean animation is mistaken for observational evidence. **Check or fix:** Label the model assumptions and identify where realism is intentionally omitted. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational, 3d, vector.

- **AVQ-027-011** Does a conserved quantity remain consistent across every displayed step when the lesson depends on conservation? **Failure hypothesis:** Attractive animation creates or destroys the quantity it claims to track. **Check or fix:** Reconcile a stepwise ledger with every relevant frame state. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-012** Are interpolation and extrapolation visually distinguishable from actual observed data? **Failure hypothesis:** Smooth curves imply measurements or predictions beyond the evidence. **Check or fix:** Mark observed points and clearly identify modeled extensions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-013** Does the graph's axis choice preserve the comparison the narration claims? **Failure hypothesis:** Truncation or nonlinear scaling exaggerates the apparent effect. **Check or fix:** Replot with disclosed alternatives and review the claimed interpretation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-014** Is any simulated motion being described as a validated physical result without a suitable model check? **Failure hypothesis:** Visual plausibility is treated as evidence of physical accuracy. **Check or fix:** Separate illustrative animation from validated simulation claims. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production. **Style filters:** educational, 3d.

- **AVQ-027-015** Does a mathematically correct example rely on assumptions absent from the on-screen problem? **Failure hypothesis:** The calculation solves a different problem than the viewer is given. **Check or fix:** List assumptions and reconcile them with the stated setup. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-027-016** Do all displayed algorithm outputs come from the exact algorithm version being described? **Failure hypothesis:** Explanatory graphics show hand-edited or stale results. **Check or fix:** Regenerate outputs from the pinned implementation and compare them. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-027-017** Has the algorithm been checked on boundary inputs rather than only the attractive demonstration case? **Failure hypothesis:** Empty, singleton, extreme, or invalid inputs expose unmentioned failure behavior. **Check or fix:** Define the valid domain and test its meaningful boundaries. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-018** Are tie-breaking and ordering rules explicit when they can change the visible result? **Failure hypothesis:** An apparently unique answer depends on an unstated arbitrary order. **Check or fix:** Declare the rule and test tied or permuted inputs. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-027-019** Can numerical rounding or tolerance choices flip a displayed classification or comparison? **Failure hypothesis:** Near-boundary values produce unstable or misleading decisions. **Check or fix:** Test values around thresholds and expose relevant tolerance rules. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-020** Does random generation enforce the promised constraints for every output rather than merely most seeds? **Failure hypothesis:** A successful sample is treated as proof of a universal invariant. **Check or fix:** Test and validate each generated instance against the explicit constraints. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-027-021** Is the chosen demonstration seed representative rather than selected to hide common failures? **Failure hypothesis:** Cherry-picked outputs exaggerate reliability or quality. **Check or fix:** Inspect a declared sample set and report failures alongside the selected case. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, production. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-022** Does the on-screen algorithm explanation account for failure or nontermination conditions that matter in use? **Failure hypothesis:** A happy-path animation conceals an unsafe or unusable edge behavior. **Check or fix:** Identify relevant failure states and demonstrate or disclose them. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, script, production. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-027-023** Are test results described only as evidence for the specific assertions the tests actually evaluate? **Failure hypothesis:** Unit-test success is inflated into factual or production approval. **Check or fix:** Map every success claim to test scope and untested dimensions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-027-024** Are proposed capabilities clearly separated from capabilities demonstrated by a retained output? **Failure hypothesis:** Plans and documentation are reported as completed functionality. **Check or fix:** Require a concrete artifact and check record for each demonstrated claim. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-027-025** Are counts of recipes, styles, stories, and tested videos using distinct definitions? **Failure hypothesis:** Combinatorial variants are presented as independent authored or tested works. **Check or fix:** Publish separate counts with explicit counting units. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-027-026** Does a performance claim include the actual environment, input size, and end-to-end work measured? **Failure hypothesis:** Selective timing omits setup, review, retries, or costly stages. **Check or fix:** Record the complete measured workflow under stated conditions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-027-027** Is a cost-saving claim based on actual charges and a comparable baseline? **Failure hypothesis:** Estimates or omitted cloud costs are presented as demonstrated savings. **Check or fix:** Reconcile invoices and measured effort with the comparison workflow. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-027-028** Are safety-critical instructions blocked from authoritative presentation until appropriate current sources resolve them? **Failure hypothesis:** Plausible but unchecked advice could cause real harm. **Check or fix:** Obtain suitable current primary-source review and hold unresolved instructions. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-029** Do conflicting reliable sources remain visible as an unresolved issue rather than being silently averaged into a claim? **Failure hypothesis:** Source disagreement is hidden behind false certainty. **Check or fix:** Record the conflict, scope, and dates and narrow the claim if possible. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-030** Does the final voice, caption, and graphic all state the same corrected fact after a script revision? **Failure hypothesis:** A correction updates one channel while stale variants survive elsewhere. **Check or fix:** Reconcile all delivered channels against the corrected claim ledger. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-027-031** Is a reconstructed, generated, or illustrative scene clearly distinguished from documentary evidence? **Failure hypothesis:** Synthetic imagery appears to prove an event or observation. **Check or fix:** Label the representation and cite the actual evidence separately. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, live-action, 3d.

- **AVQ-027-032** Does the claim of interactivity correspond to actual user-controllable behavior rather than a prerecorded question? **Failure hypothesis:** A passive video is marketed as interactive software. **Check or fix:** Demonstrate the claimed input-response path or describe the work accurately. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle, screen-recording.

### D028 rendering and export

- **AVQ-028-001** Does the final render contain every intended frame exactly once and in the correct order? **Failure hypothesis:** Batch rendering omits, duplicates, or misorders frames. **Check or fix:** Compare the frame sequence against the expected index manifest. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-002** Are nominally adjacent render chunks evaluating animation at the same shared time authority? **Failure hypothesis:** Local chunk clocks create motion jumps at boundaries. **Check or fix:** Render boundary overlaps and compare identical-time frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-003** Is the export using final-resolution assets rather than proxies or preview caches? **Failure hypothesis:** Low-resolution working material survives a successful render. **Check or fix:** Audit resolved asset paths and inspect final-scale texture/detail crops. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-004** Do the output dimensions and pixel aspect ratio produce the intended display geometry? **Failure hypothesis:** Correct pixel counts still yield stretched or squeezed playback. **Check or fix:** Inspect sample/display aspect metadata and compare a known geometric reference. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-005** Is the chosen crop preserving every required subject and label throughout the full camera move? **Failure hypothesis:** Static framing checks miss content lost at movement extremes. **Check or fix:** Scan the delivered frame bounds against protected regions over time. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-006** Does the color-management path apply the display transform exactly once? **Failure hypothesis:** Missing or doubled transforms alter contrast and color. **Check or fix:** Trace source, working, and output transforms and compare reference patches. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-007** Are HDR sources and SDR output being mapped without losing important highlight or shadow information? **Failure hypothesis:** Automatic tone mapping hides detail or changes intended hierarchy. **Check or fix:** Inspect controlled reference scenes through the actual output transform. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-008** Do saturated colors survive output conversion without clipping away distinctions needed for the task? **Failure hypothesis:** Gamut conversion merges separate color categories. **Check or fix:** Compare important color regions before and after export conversion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-028-009** Does limited bit depth introduce visible banding in gradients that were smooth in the working render? **Failure hypothesis:** Quantization creates contour steps after export. **Check or fix:** Inspect final encoded gradients at ordinary and stressed viewing conditions. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-010** Are transparent edges composited with the correct premultiplied or straight-alpha interpretation? **Failure hypothesis:** Alpha mismatch creates dark or bright halos around cutouts. **Check or fix:** Composite edge tests over light and dark backgrounds. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** cutout, vector, 3d.

- **AVQ-028-011** Does motion blur erase a puzzle target, contact event, or technical detail needed to verify the action? **Failure hypothesis:** A cinematic shutter setting destroys required evidence. **Check or fix:** Review the decisive frames and lower blur selectively where needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-028-012** Are temporal denoising or upscaling leaving ghost trails around moving silhouettes? **Failure hypothesis:** History-based processing blends incompatible object positions. **Check or fix:** Inspect moving edges across consecutive final frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, live-action.

- **AVQ-028-013** Does the sample count support stable shadows and reflections rather than flickering noise? **Failure hypothesis:** Undersampling turns static surface appearance into distracting temporal variation. **Check or fix:** Render a short motion test at candidate settings and compare stability. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d.

- **AVQ-028-014** Are fine patterns and linework stable under motion after rasterization? **Failure hypothesis:** Spatial detail exceeds the sampling rate and produces shimmer or moire. **Check or fix:** Inspect full-speed output and adjust filtering or detail frequency. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** vector, 3d, live-action.

- **AVQ-028-015** Does frame interpolation invent malformed intermediate geometry or duplicate a decisive event? **Failure hypothesis:** Synthetic in-between frames distort the action. **Check or fix:** Inspect interpolated intervals around occlusions and contact. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-016** Are cached simulations and rendered layers from the same approved scene revision? **Failure hypothesis:** Stale caches combine old motion with new geometry or timing. **Check or fix:** Reconcile cache hashes and invalidate mismatched dependencies. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d.

- **AVQ-028-017** Are all required render passes actually included in the final composite? **Failure hypothesis:** A successful beauty export omits masks, shadows, or corrective passes. **Check or fix:** Compare the pass manifest to the compositing graph and final image. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, cutout.

- **AVQ-028-018** Does the export accidentally include guides, bounding boxes, debug text, or temporary watermarks? **Failure hypothesis:** Review overlays remain enabled in the delivery render. **Check or fix:** Scan the full encoded file for nonfinal layers and markers. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-019** Are procedural expressions and animated effects evaluated correctly by the final renderer rather than only the editor? **Failure hypothesis:** The export path silently drops unsupported runtime behavior. **Check or fix:** Compare representative editor and final-render frames at effect extremes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** vector, typographic, 3d.

- **AVQ-028-020** Does final depth of field keep necessary labels, clues, and causal contacts identifiable? **Failure hypothesis:** Preview sharpness differs from the final camera's focus treatment. **Check or fix:** Inspect final focus at each required observation interval. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** 3d, live-action.

- **AVQ-028-021** Is the export frame rate the approved rate rather than a default inherited from another project? **Failure hypothesis:** A preset silently changes duration or motion cadence. **Check or fix:** Verify nominal and actual frame timestamps against the project contract. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-022** Does the render range include the intended final resolved pose without appending an accidental blank or repeated frame? **Failure hypothesis:** Inclusive/exclusive endpoint confusion damages the ending. **Check or fix:** Compare requested frame range with decoded first and last frames. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-023** Are color-range flags consistent with the actual encoded signal levels? **Failure hypothesis:** Full/limited-range mismatch produces washed-out or crushed output. **Check or fix:** Decode with verified interpretation and inspect reference levels. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-024** Is the pixel format supported by the intended playback route while retaining required visual detail? **Failure hypothesis:** A technically valid export is unsuitable for destination decoding or chroma detail. **Check or fix:** Test the exact format in destination players and inspect critical edges. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-025** Do subtitles, graphics, and audio belong to the same language and revision in a batch export? **Failure hypothesis:** Reused batch variables combine incompatible version components. **Check or fix:** Verify each package against its language/revision manifest. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-026** Does the final duration satisfy the requested range without padded silence or black frames? **Failure hypothesis:** A duration target is met by filler instead of a coherent edit. **Check or fix:** Measure runtime and review the opening and closing content intervals. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-027** Can high-compression output still preserve the smallest essential target and distinction? **Failure hypothesis:** File-size optimization defeats the video's core viewing task. **Check or fix:** Compare candidate encodes at intended viewing size using the actual task. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-028-028** Does export metadata accidentally mark the file as rotated, stereoscopic, or panoramic when it is ordinary flat video? **Failure hypothesis:** Inherited metadata causes unintended player transformations. **Check or fix:** Inspect relevant flags and verify plain playback geometry. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-029** Is the encoded file truly generated from the reviewed source rather than an older render with a new filename? **Failure hypothesis:** Renaming conceals stale content while appearing current. **Check or fix:** Verify source/render hashes and compare changed landmarks. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-030** Are separate render jobs contaminating one another through shared temporary files or output paths? **Failure hypothesis:** Concurrent jobs overwrite assets or frames silently. **Check or fix:** Use isolated job directories and reconcile per-job output hashes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-031** Does an interrupted export leave a partial file that could be mistaken for a complete deliverable? **Failure hypothesis:** File existence is treated as render success. **Check or fix:** Require successful completion plus full decode and duration validation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-028-032** Are final-render quality claims supported by viewing the whole encoded result rather than selected attractive stills? **Failure hypothesis:** Still-frame inspection misses motion, transition, and temporal defects. **Check or fix:** Watch the full delivered encode and record timecoded findings. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode. **Style filters:** any.

### D029 decoding and delivery

- **AVQ-029-001** Does the delivered filename's extension agree with the actual media container and streams? **Failure hypothesis:** A renamed file is mistaken for a valid export in the promised format. **Check or fix:** Inspect file signatures and stream metadata rather than trusting the name. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-002** Are the downloaded bytes identical to the approved deliverable when no destination transcoding is intended? **Failure hypothesis:** Transfer or packaging corruption changes the approved file. **Check or fix:** Compare cryptographic hashes before and after the real download path. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-003** Can the complete delivered file be decoded without errors, including its final frames? **Failure hypothesis:** Header inspection misses corruption late in the media stream. **Check or fix:** Run a full decode and review every reported error. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-004** Does the exact codec profile and level play on the lowest supported device? **Failure hypothesis:** General codec support is mistaken for support of every profile and resolution. **Check or fix:** Test the delivered file on the stated minimum device/player. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-005** Is hardware acceleration required for smooth playback on the intended audience's devices? **Failure hypothesis:** A demanding encode stutters on realistic decoding hardware. **Check or fix:** Measure dropped frames and responsiveness on representative devices. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-006** Can progressive playback begin promptly without downloading the entire file first? **Failure hypothesis:** Container layout delays access to essential playback metadata. **Check or fix:** Test a fresh download over the expected connection and inspect startup behavior. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-007** Does the video remain useful under the destination's lowest likely adaptive rendition? **Failure hypothesis:** Automatic quality reduction erases critical text or puzzle details. **Check or fix:** Inspect the actual low-bitrate rendition using the viewer's task. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** educational, puzzle.

- **AVQ-029-008** Does constrained bandwidth cause stalls precisely during the solving or decision interval? **Failure hypothesis:** Buffer interruptions alter the intended experience and available response time. **Check or fix:** Test realistic constrained playback and provide a suitable download option if needed. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-009** Are rotation and display-orientation flags interpreted consistently by target players? **Failure hypothesis:** Different players rotate or letterbox the same pixels differently. **Check or fix:** Compare the file on the supported player set. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-010** Does the selected thumbnail reveal a puzzle answer or narrative surprise before playback? **Failure hypothesis:** Automatically chosen preview imagery spoils the experience. **Check or fix:** Review the actual destination thumbnail and select an appropriate authorized replacement. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle, fiction.

- **AVQ-029-011** Is the intended audio track selected by default rather than commentary, a stem, or another language? **Failure hypothesis:** Track ordering or metadata causes the wrong listening experience. **Check or fix:** Open the final file fresh in target players and listen to the default track. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, auditory. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-012** Does muted autoplay still make the opening intelligible enough for the viewer to choose whether to continue? **Failure hypothesis:** The initial experience depends on audio the platform suppresses. **Check or fix:** Test actual muted autoplay and make the opening promise visible. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-013** Are caption sidecars actually linked and available to the recipient rather than merely sitting beside the source file? **Failure hypothesis:** Captions exist in storage but are absent from the delivery player. **Check or fix:** Open the recipient-facing player and enable the supplied captions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-014** Does switching language tracks select a matching caption track where the player supports that behavior? **Failure hypothesis:** Independent track defaults produce mismatched spoken and written languages. **Check or fix:** Test the available language combinations explicitly. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-015** Has destination transcoding finished before the video is described as ready for review? **Failure hypothesis:** A placeholder or incomplete low-quality rendition is mistaken for final delivery. **Check or fix:** Verify processing completion and inspect the resulting rendition. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-016** Does playback reach the intended ending rather than stop early at a bad duration or timestamp boundary? **Failure hypothesis:** Container timing makes a valid tail inaccessible to the player. **Check or fix:** Play through the end and compare visible duration with decoded content. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-017** Can a viewer seek to a critical explanation without waiting excessively or landing on corrupted frames? **Failure hypothesis:** Sparse or malformed random-access points impair navigation. **Check or fix:** Seek repeatedly around key sections on target players. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-018** Does loop playback include an unintended pause, black flash, or duplicate frame imposed by the destination player? **Failure hypothesis:** Local seamlessness is broken by destination loop behavior. **Check or fix:** Observe multiple actual destination loops and adjust delivery if possible. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-019** Do player controls or captions supplied by the destination cover evidence placed near the frame edge? **Failure hypothesis:** Delivery UI obscures content that was safe in the clean export. **Check or fix:** Inspect the real player with controls and captions visible. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-020** Does automatic platform cropping preserve the intended composition in every supported presentation slot? **Failure hypothesis:** Feed, preview, or full-screen modes use different crops. **Check or fix:** Test each actual slot and prepare separately reviewed variants where needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-021** Are the file's access settings limited to the audience the user authorized? **Failure hypothesis:** Delivery convenience accidentally makes a private film publicly reachable. **Check or fix:** Inspect sharing scope before sending and verify the intended recipient view. **Severity:** critical. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-022** Can the intended recipient open the delivered attachment or link with their actual permissions? **Failure hypothesis:** Owner access is mistaken for recipient access. **Check or fix:** Verify recipient access without broadening permissions beyond authorization. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-023** Will the access link remain valid for the expected review period? **Failure hypothesis:** An expiring transfer fails before reviewers can use it. **Check or fix:** Check expiry against the agreed review window and choose a suitable authorized route. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-024** Does destination caching continue serving an older rendition after the approved file has been replaced? **Failure hypothesis:** A cached media response defeats an otherwise correct version update. **Check or fix:** Compare fresh-session playback and downloaded rendition identity with the approved replacement. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-025** Does the media metadata expose unintended location, personal names, internal paths, or production notes? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden metadata leaks information absent from the visible film. **Check or fix:** Audit metadata and remove unnecessary private fields from the delivery copy. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-026** Is a compatibility fallback the same approved revision as the primary file? **Failure hypothesis:** A playable alternate silently delivers an older story or fact. **Check or fix:** Compare version manifests and content landmarks across all variants. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-027** Is the playable film clearly separated from source archives so the recipient does not have to unpack a project to watch it? **Failure hypothesis:** Packaging makes a technically complete delivery unusable for its audience. **Check or fix:** Provide a clearly identified playback file alongside any approved source package. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-028** Are the delivery instructions naming a genuinely available file rather than a private build path? **Failure hypothesis:** The handoff points to storage the recipient cannot reach. **Check or fix:** Follow every recipient-facing link or attachment path from the recipient context. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-029** Does an embedded player require third-party cookies or an account that the intended audience may not have? **Failure hypothesis:** Authentication or browser privacy constraints block otherwise valid media. **Check or fix:** Test the expected signed-out or restricted-browser context. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-030** Does the destination recompress audio in a way that introduces artifacts or changes speech intelligibility? **Failure hypothesis:** Source audio review does not cover the platform's actual rendition. **Check or fix:** Listen to the processed destination audio on representative devices. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-031** Are review comments attached to an immutable version so later replacement cannot change what was approved? **Failure hypothesis:** A moving link makes prior feedback and signoff ambiguous. **Check or fix:** Preserve version IDs or hashes in the review record. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-029-032** Can a recipient tell which file is the final intended version when several drafts or aspect ratios are present? **Failure hypothesis:** Ambiguous naming leads to review or distribution of the wrong asset. **Check or fix:** Use a clear manifest identifying status, purpose, and approved version. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** delivery. **Style filters:** any.

### D030 audience value

- **AVQ-030-001** What concrete thing should the intended viewer understand, feel, or be able to do after this video? **Failure hypothesis:** A vague audience goal prevents meaningful evaluation of success. **Check or fix:** Write one observable viewer outcome before judging the cut. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-002** Can first-time viewers state the video's central point without being told the creator's intention? **Failure hypothesis:** Production familiarity is mistaken for communicated meaning. **Check or fix:** Collect unprompted summaries after a single complete viewing. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-003** Does the film assume background knowledge its intended audience actually lacks? **Failure hypothesis:** Unstated prerequisites make the explanation inaccessible to novices. **Check or fix:** Test the premise with representative novices and introduce necessary prerequisites. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-004** Is there enough specific value for an experienced viewer, or only an elaborate statement of the obvious? **Failure hypothesis:** Visual polish disguises a trivial takeaway. **Check or fix:** Ask informed viewers what new useful distinction they gained. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, animatic. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-005** Does the opening promise accurately describe the value the rest of the film delivers? **Failure hypothesis:** A compelling hook attracts viewers to a different or weaker result. **Check or fix:** Compare the hook with the actual demonstrated takeaway. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-006** Can viewers apply the lesson to a new example rather than merely repeat the shown answer? **Failure hypothesis:** Recognition is mistaken for transferable understanding. **Check or fix:** Present a different appropriate case after viewing. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-007** Do viewers remember the essential point after a short delay without replaying the film? **Failure hypothesis:** Immediate fluency masks fragile retention. **Check or fix:** Use a delayed recall check matched to the intended use. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-008** Does the video save a user measurable effort compared with the manual workflow it claims to improve? **Failure hypothesis:** Claimed convenience ignores setup, corrections, and review effort. **Check or fix:** Compare complete workflows on the same realistic task. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-030-009** Is the benefit still present when the user must correct the output's common errors? **Failure hypothesis:** A fast first result creates more downstream repair work. **Check or fix:** Include observed repair effort in the usefulness comparison. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-030-010** Can viewers identify what remains uncertain or unproven after watching? **Failure hypothesis:** Persuasive presentation creates confidence beyond the evidence. **Check or fix:** Ask viewers which claims were demonstrated and which remain open. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-011** Does the piece respect the viewing situation, such as interruption, background noise, or a small screen? **Failure hypothesis:** A design that works in a quiet review room fails in normal use. **Check or fix:** Test the actual intended context and prioritize its essential information. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-012** Would a viewer choose to watch the full runtime for the value delivered, without being obliged to review it? **Failure hypothesis:** Creator interest is mistaken for audience attention. **Check or fix:** Observe voluntary stopping points and ask what value remained unclear. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-013** Does the film provide a reason to care before requesting effortful interpretation? **Failure hypothesis:** Audience labor is demanded before a relevant benefit is established. **Check or fix:** Introduce a concrete stake or practical question early. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, animatic. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-014** Are emotional reactions based on the story rather than pressure from manipulative framing or exaggerated claims? **Failure hypothesis:** Presentation extracts a response without earning trust. **Check or fix:** Ask viewers what specifically caused their reaction and check supporting content. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-015** Does the film's novelty come from a useful idea or experience rather than only a different visual skin? **Failure hypothesis:** Surface variation is counted as additional audience value. **Check or fix:** Compare takeaways and experiences across related films without style labels. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-016** Can a viewer distinguish the intended subject from decorative spectacle when asked what mattered? **Failure hypothesis:** High production density competes with the actual point. **Check or fix:** Collect salience rankings and reduce low-value dominant elements. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-017** Does the video make a difficult concept easier to reason about than a simpler static explanation would? **Failure hypothesis:** Motion adds complexity without explanatory benefit. **Check or fix:** Compare comprehension using the video and a concise static alternative. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, animatic. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-018** Is the format appropriate to the viewer's task, such as learning a process versus checking a reference fact? **Failure hypothesis:** A linear film is used for information viewers need to scan or revisit. **Check or fix:** Observe task behavior and add a suitable companion reference if warranted. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, delivery, edit. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-019** Does the conclusion leave the viewer with a feasible next step when action is the purpose? **Failure hypothesis:** The film motivates action without explaining what can actually be done. **Check or fix:** Ask viewers to name their next step and remove practical ambiguity. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-020** Are effort, tools, prerequisites, and costs disclosed when viewers are encouraged to replicate a result? **Failure hypothesis:** A polished demonstration hides barriers that affect usefulness. **Check or fix:** Itemize the actual replication requirements beside the demonstrated outcome. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** script, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-030-021** Can viewers recognize the limits of the example instead of generalizing it to unsuitable situations? **Failure hypothesis:** A memorable example becomes an overbroad rule. **Check or fix:** Test a boundary case and make the relevant limitation explicit. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-022** Does humor rely on cultural knowledge the intended audience is likely to share? **Failure hypothesis:** The setup is invisible to viewers outside the creator's reference group. **Check or fix:** Test the joke with relevant audience groups and revise missing context. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction.

- **AVQ-030-023** Are potentially alienating depictions necessary to the film's purpose and understood as intended? **Failure hypothesis:** Unexamined stereotypes distract or harm trust. **Check or fix:** Seek relevant perspective and revise unsupported representational shortcuts. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-024** Does the film reward attention with a satisfying resolution rather than an engagement bait that never pays off? **Failure hypothesis:** A question or promise exists only to prolong viewing. **Check or fix:** Identify the delivered payoff and test whether viewers feel the promise was met. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-025** Do actual audience findings remain separate from synthetic reviewer suggestions in the report? **Failure hypothesis:** Generated opinions are presented as human research. **Check or fix:** Label the origin of every review and report human sample details honestly. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** animatic, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-026** Is the audience sample relevant to the intended users rather than only the production team? **Failure hypothesis:** Familiar or expert reviewers miss novice misunderstandings. **Check or fix:** Document participant relevance and recruit appropriate review when authorized. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-027** Does the evaluation question measure the intended outcome instead of asking only whether viewers liked the video? **Failure hypothesis:** Satisfaction is mistaken for comprehension, accuracy, or usefulness. **Check or fix:** Match the question and task to the stated success criterion. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, animatic. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-030-028** Are observed improvements large enough to matter in the viewer's real task? **Failure hypothesis:** A measurable change is framed as valuable despite negligible practical effect. **Check or fix:** Define a task-relevant benefit threshold and compare actual outcomes. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-030-029** Does a polished before-and-after comparison hold task difficulty and input quality constant? **Failure hypothesis:** Unfair conditions exaggerate the apparent value of the approach. **Check or fix:** Match inputs and conditions and disclose unavoidable differences. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, animatic, delivery. **Style filters:** educational, screen-recording.

- **AVQ-030-030** Is the viewer asked to do more work than the payoff justifies, such as reading dense setup for a minor joke? **Failure hypothesis:** Participation cost exceeds the value of the experience. **Check or fix:** Observe effort and confusion, then simplify setup or strengthen payoff. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, animatic. **Style filters:** fiction, puzzle.

- **AVQ-030-031** Can skeptical viewers locate the evidence behind the video's strongest claim? **Failure hypothesis:** Credibility depends on production authority rather than inspectable support. **Check or fix:** Provide a clear evidence route appropriate to the delivery format. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** script, delivery. **Style filters:** educational.

- **AVQ-030-032** Does repeating the viewing reveal useful detail or merely expose the lack of a clear first-view explanation? **Failure hypothesis:** Required replay is mislabeled as replay value. **Check or fix:** Ask what prompted replay and distinguish enrichment from repair. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

### D031 puzzles and fairness

- **AVQ-031-001** Does the stated rule define exactly what counts as a target, including partial, stylized, or obscured examples? **Failure hypothesis:** Solvers apply different reasonable category boundaries. **Check or fix:** Write explicit inclusion criteria and test ambiguous instances. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-002** Can every counted target be identified at ordinary viewing size in the actual delivery encode? **Failure hypothesis:** Technical degradation makes nominally present targets practically unknowable. **Check or fix:** Test all targets at destination size without zooming. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-003** Are target-like decorations excluded, removed, or clearly distinguishable from valid targets? **Failure hypothesis:** Incidental shapes create an equally reasonable alternate count. **Check or fix:** Audit the whole image for unmanifested target-like forms. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-004** Do reflections, shadows, pictures-within-pictures, or repeated displays have a stated counting rule? **Failure hypothesis:** Representation layers are confused with additional physical targets. **Check or fix:** Declare the rule before solving and apply it consistently. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-005** Is the answer unique under every interpretation allowed by the written rules? **Failure hypothesis:** The puzzle has multiple valid solutions despite a single-answer reveal. **Check or fix:** Enumerate or independently reason through admissible solutions. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-006** Does the reveal use the same scene and rules the solver actually saw? **Failure hypothesis:** The answer is justified by altered evidence or a revised definition. **Check or fix:** Compare puzzle and reveal states before adding annotations. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-007** Can the answer be proven from visible evidence without relying on hidden object IDs or creator knowledge? **Failure hypothesis:** Internal scene metadata supplies information unavailable to viewers. **Check or fix:** Ask an independent solver to justify the answer from the delivered video alone. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-008** Are targets that leave the frame counted consistently with the declared observation window? **Failure hypothesis:** A moving-camera puzzle silently changes whether off-screen objects count. **Check or fix:** Define the window and whether counting is instantaneous or cumulative. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-009** Can solvers track a target through motion without counting it again when it reappears? **Failure hypothesis:** Identity loss through cuts or occlusion inflates cumulative counts. **Check or fix:** Preserve distinctive identity or redesign motion to avoid ambiguous reentry. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, animatic, production, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-010** Does each valid target receive enough unobscured exposure to be found within the promised solving time? **Failure hypothesis:** Brief technical visibility is mistaken for a fair observation opportunity. **Check or fix:** Measure clear exposure and verify detection with new solvers. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-011** Are the hardest targets difficult because of intended reasoning or search, rather than blur, compression, or unreadable size? **Failure hypothesis:** The challenge depends on production defects rather than puzzle design. **Check or fix:** Compare clean and delivered versions and remove degradation-dependent difficulty. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-012** Does an occluded target expose enough diagnostic features to justify its classification? **Failure hypothesis:** Solvers must guess what is behind a foreground object. **Check or fix:** Identify the visible evidence for membership or increase fair exposure. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-013** Are similarly drawn species or categories distinguishable under the puzzle's actual target definition? **Failure hypothesis:** Stylization erases the trait needed to separate valid and invalid objects. **Check or fix:** Test category recognition independently of the counting task. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-014** Does a size-ordering puzzle specify whether solvers should compare apparent image size or implied real-world size? **Failure hypothesis:** Perspective creates two reasonable size rankings under an unstated metric. **Check or fix:** State the comparison metric and verify the answer using that metric. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-015** Does the puzzle require outside knowledge that the premise presents as a purely visual search? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden trivia changes the task without warning. **Check or fix:** State the knowledge requirement or supply the needed information. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-016** Does the wording leak the intended answer when the task is meant to require discovery? **Failure hypothesis:** A title, count label, or grammatical cue gives away the solution. **Check or fix:** Read all visible text before viewing the puzzle and check for answer leakage. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-017** Do relational clues still identify the intended objects after mirroring or reframing for another layout? **Failure hypothesis:** Left-right or relative-position clues become false in a transformed version. **Check or fix:** Solve each delivered layout independently and revise directional clues where needed. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** edit, final-encode, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-018** Is an impossible instance allowed by the prompt, or does the wording guarantee a solution that may not exist? **Failure hypothesis:** Contradictory constraints force solvers to search indefinitely for a nonexistent answer. **Check or fix:** Check satisfiability and state explicitly when 'no solution' is an admissible response. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-019** Can pausing or replaying change the challenge in a way that should be acknowledged in the instructions? **Failure hypothesis:** A timed challenge implies control over playback it does not possess. **Check or fix:** Describe the intended play convention without claiming enforced interactivity. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** script, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-020** Are optional hints ordered so each helps without accidentally giving away the entire solution? **Failure hypothesis:** The first hint collapses the task while later hints add nothing. **Check or fix:** Test hint-by-hint solving and revise the information progression. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-021** Does the answer reveal identify every counted target exactly once? **Failure hypothesis:** Highlighting omits, duplicates, or skips targets while displaying a plausible total. **Check or fix:** Reconcile reveal IDs and visible markers with the count manifest. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-022** Do reveal outlines remain registered to moving targets under the final camera transform? **Failure hypothesis:** Annotations point at empty space or neighboring distractors. **Check or fix:** Inspect marker-to-target alignment across the full reveal. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, edit, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-023** Is the clean solving view free from faint reveal masks, numbering, or residual answer overlays? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden or partially transparent solution layers leak assistance. **Check or fix:** Inspect exported solving frames with the intended contrast and brightness. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, final-encode. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-024** Can a target enter or leave the valid set during animation without an explicit rule for that state change? **Failure hypothesis:** Morphing, opening, or orientation changes make the answer time-dependent. **Check or fix:** Freeze membership or define when and how it changes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-025** Does random layout generation preserve solution uniqueness and fair spacing for this specific instance? **Failure hypothesis:** Generic generator constraints fail on the selected arrangement. **Check or fix:** Validate the delivered instance and independently solve it. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-026** Are distractors wrong for a visible reason rather than an arbitrary creator-only exception? **Failure hypothesis:** The answer depends on an unstated special case. **Check or fix:** Require an observable exclusion reason for each close distractor. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-027** Does a visual pattern have a defensible next step rather than several equally simple continuations? **Failure hypothesis:** A preferred pattern is presented as mathematically compelled. **Check or fix:** Compare alternative rules and narrow the prompt where necessary. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** concept, script, storyboard. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-028** Are number puzzles robust to rounding, operation-order, and notation conventions used by the audience? **Failure hypothesis:** Different standard conventions yield different answers. **Check or fix:** State conventions and independently solve the displayed notation. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, production. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-029** If an audio clue is essential, is that requirement clear before muted viewers begin solving? **Failure hypothesis:** A seemingly visual puzzle quietly depends on unheard information. **Check or fix:** Listen to identify essential audio clues and label the required modality. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, auditory, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-030** Are example solutions consistent with the actual rules rather than teaching an exception that never returns? **Failure hypothesis:** The tutorial primes a strategy the main puzzle rejects. **Check or fix:** Apply every example's reasoning to the final instance. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** script, storyboard, animatic. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-031** Does the difficulty claim reflect actual cold solving rather than the creator's familiarity with the answer? **Failure hypothesis:** Insider knowledge distorts both estimated difficulty and allotted time. **Check or fix:** Test with unbriefed relevant solvers and report the limited sample honestly. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** human-audience. **Stages:** animatic, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle.

- **AVQ-031-032** Does the reveal explain why plausible alternative answers fail, rather than merely displaying the preferred number? **Failure hypothesis:** A declarative answer leaves legitimate ambiguity unresolved. **Check or fix:** Address the strongest alternate interpretation with visible evidence. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** visual, human-audience. **Stages:** script, animatic, edit. **Style filters:** puzzle.

### D032 reproducibility and maintenance

- **AVQ-032-001** Can another authorized machine open the native project without relying on the creator's private absolute paths? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden local paths make the handoff nonportable. **Check or fix:** Reopen from a clean location and repair unresolved relative dependencies. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-002** Are all required source assets present at the exact versions used for the approved render? **Failure hypothesis:** Similar filenames conceal missing or changed source bytes. **Check or fix:** Verify a complete asset manifest against cryptographic hashes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-003** Is the actual renderer, application, plugin, and model version recorded rather than only a generic tool name? **Failure hypothesis:** Environment drift prevents reproducing the approved appearance. **Check or fix:** Capture the versions used by the successful build. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-004** Does the project declare which dependencies are installed separately because they cannot be bundled? **Failure hypothesis:** A supposedly self-contained package omits essential external components. **Check or fix:** Separate included assets from required external dependencies and acquisition steps. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-005** Can the render be reproduced using the documented command without undocumented manual editor state? **Failure hypothesis:** Hidden UI settings or selection state determine the output. **Check or fix:** Rebuild from a fresh session following only the handoff. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-006** Are random seeds and generation inputs retained wherever stochastic output affects the film? **Failure hypothesis:** Re-running changes placement, motion, or generated assets unpredictably. **Check or fix:** Save seeds, exact inputs, and generated source bytes. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-007** Does the workflow distinguish reproducible procedural assets from generation steps that cannot be rerun identically? **Failure hypothesis:** A prompt is treated as sufficient to recreate nondeterministic output. **Check or fix:** Preserve exact generated files and state the repeatability boundary. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-008** Are scene units, coordinate conventions, and frame-rate assumptions explicit in the handoff? **Failure hypothesis:** Reopening or importing changes scale, orientation, or time interpretation. **Check or fix:** Record conventions and verify a known reference object and event. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-009** Is there one authoritative timeline and scene transform for camera, targets, masks, and audio? **Failure hypothesis:** Parallel timing or transform systems drift when the scene changes. **Check or fix:** Map shared authorities and test a controlled timing/camera edit. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-010** Can a maintainer change one character or prop without breaking unrelated layers or hidden references? **Failure hypothesis:** Tight coupling turns a local edit into widespread damage. **Check or fix:** Perform a bounded substitution in a copy and inspect dependent outputs. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-011** Do layer names and ownership describe real independently controllable roles rather than arbitrary duplicates? **Failure hypothesis:** Inflated layer counts conceal an unmaintainable monolithic composition. **Check or fix:** Audit each layer's purpose and demonstrate meaningful isolation. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-012** Are the clean puzzle, reveal annotations, captions, and audio separable for future revisions? **Failure hypothesis:** Flattened deliverables force destructive edits for routine updates. **Check or fix:** Verify editable source separation for the promised revision types. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** puzzle, typographic.

- **AVQ-032-013** Does changing a source asset invalidate all caches that depend on it? **Failure hypothesis:** A render appears updated while stale cached geometry or frames persist. **Check or fix:** Alter a controlled fixture and trace cache invalidation through the pipeline. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-014** Are generated intermediates distinguishable from authoritative source files? **Failure hypothesis:** A maintainer edits disposable output and loses changes on regeneration. **Check or fix:** Label source-of-truth files and document regeneration boundaries. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-015** Can a failed long render resume without silently skipping or mixing frames from another revision? **Failure hypothesis:** Resume logic treats stale partial output as valid progress. **Check or fix:** Test interruption and restart with per-frame provenance checks. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-016** Does the build fail clearly when an asset, font, or plugin is missing instead of silently substituting it? **Failure hypothesis:** Fallback behavior produces plausible but incorrect output. **Check or fix:** Remove a controlled dependency in a test copy and inspect the failure mode. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-017** Are configuration defaults safe when a new episode omits a required field? **Failure hypothesis:** An inherited default silently supplies the wrong story, count, or export setting. **Check or fix:** Validate required fields and test intentionally incomplete inputs. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-018** Are output filenames and directories derived from stable project/version identifiers rather than mutable titles alone? **Failure hypothesis:** Renaming or duplicate titles overwrite unrelated work. **Check or fix:** Test collision cases and include stable IDs in the build manifest. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-019** Can automated checks detect a deliberately wrong target count, missing frame, or stale caption file? **Failure hypothesis:** Green test reports prove only that the test runner executed. **Check or fix:** Seed representative known-wrong fixtures and verify expected failures. **Severity:** high. **Modes:** machine. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-020** Can a maintainer recover why the current creative choice was selected over the rejected alternatives? **Failure hypothesis:** Missing decision history causes discarded approaches to be repeated as new improvements. **Check or fix:** Retain representative alternatives with dated, evidence-linked selection reasons. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, human-audience. **Stages:** production, delivery. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-021** Are visual regression comparisons tolerant of harmless variation but sensitive to meaningful defects? **Failure hypothesis:** Pixel diffs either flood reviewers with noise or miss semantic failures. **Check or fix:** Calibrate against accepted variations and known consequential defects. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.

- **AVQ-032-022** Is there a small representative fixture for each risky renderer, codec, or plugin upgrade? **Failure hypothesis:** Upgrades are tested only by rerendering the entire film after failure. **Check or fix:** Preserve minimal scenes covering contact, text, transparency, sound, and timing risks. **Severity:** medium. **Modes:** machine, visual, auditory. **Stages:** production. **Style filters:** any.
